Position\nconst positionedChildren = world.query(ChildOf(parent), Position)\n```\n\n## Traversing Graphs\n\n### Recursive traversal\n\n```typescript\nfunction traverseFromNode(world: World, node: child, depth + 1)\n  }\n}\n\n// Start from root\ntraverseFromNode(world, root)\n```\n\n### Building a tree\n\n```typescript\nfunction buildTree(world: World, parent: Entity, depth: number, maxDepth: }\n}\n\nconst root = world.spawn()\nbuildTree(world, root, 0, 4)\n```\n\n### Finding ancestors\n\n```typescript\nfunction getAncestors(entity: Entity): Entity[] {\n  const ancestors: Entity[] = []\n Relations\n\nOrdered relations maintain a list of related entities with bidirectional sync. Use when order matters (UI layers, rendering order, execution order).\n\n### Why ordered relations?\n\nA regular query returns a flat unordered list:\n\n```",

sync\nchild3.add(ChildOf(parent)) // child3 automatically added to list\n```\n\n### Supported methods\n\n**Standard array methods:**\n\n- `push(entity)` - Add to end\n- `pop()` - Remove from end\n- `shift()` - Remove from front\n- `unshift(entity)` - Add to front\n- `splice(start, deleteCount, ...items)` - Remove/insert\n\n**Special methods:**\n\n- `moveTo(entity, index)` - Move entity to OrderedChildren = ordered(ChildOf)\n\nconst scene = world.spawn(OrderedChildren)\nconst layers = scene.get(OrderedChildren)\n\nconst background = world.spawn(ChildOf(scene))\nconst gameplay = world.spawn(ChildOf(scene))\nconst ui = world.spawn(ChildOf(scene))\n\n// Render in order (background first, UI last)\nfunction render(world: World) {\n  for (const layer of layers) {\n    renderLayer(layer)\n  }\n}\n\n// Reorder dynamically\nlayers.moveTo(ui, 0) // Move UI to back\n```\n\n### Example: Execution order\n\n```typescript\nconst ChildOf = relation()\nconst OrderedSystems = ordered(ChildOf)\n\nconst pipeline = world.spawn(OrderedSystems)\nconst systems = pipeline.get(OrderedSystems)\n\n// Define system execution order\nsystems.push(inputSystem)\nsystems.push(physicsSystem)\nsystems.push(renderSystem)\n\n// Run in order\nfunction tick(world: World) {\n  for (const system of systems) {\n    executeSystem(system)\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Performance notes\n\nOrdered relations add bookkeeping overhead:\n\n- Cost is paid during structural changes (add, remove, move)\n- NOT during query/iteration time\n- Use only when order is essential\n\n**When to use:**\n\n- UI layer/z-index management\n- System execution order\n- Render order\n- Any time iteration order matters\n\n**When NOT to use:**\n\n- Order doesn\u0027t matter\n- Can sort at query time\n- Performance-critical hot paths\n\n## Removing Relations\n\n### Remove specific relation\n\n```typescript\nentity.add(Likes(apple))\nentity.add(Likes(banana))\n\nentity.remove(Likes(apple))\n\nentity.has(Likes(apple)) // false\nentity.has(Likes(banana)) // true\n```\n\n### Remove all relations of a kind (wildcard)\n\n```typescript\nentity.add(Likes(apple))\nentity.add(Likes(banana))\n\nentity.remove(Likes(\u0027*\u0027))\n\nentity.has(Likes(apple)) // false\nentity.has(Likes(banana)) // false\n```\n\n## Relation Options\n\n| Option        | Value                    | Effect                                |\n| ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------- |\n| `store`       | `{ field: default }`     | Attach data to the relation           |\n| `autoDestroy` | `\u0027orphan\u0027` or `\u0027source\u0027` | Destroy sources when target destroyed |\n| `autoDestroy` | `\u0027target\u0027`               | Destroy targets when source destroyed |\n| `exclusive`   | `true`                   | Entity can only have one target       |\n\n## React Hooks\n\n```typescript\nimport { useTarget, useTargets } from \u0027koota/react\u0027\n\n// Get first target (reactive)\nconst parent = useTarget(entity, ChildOf)\n\n// Get all targets (reactive)\nconst items = useTargets(inventory, Contains)\n```\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### \u274c Storing the parent reference manually\n\n```typescript\n// Don\u0027t do this - duplicates what relations provide\nconst Transform = trait({\n  x: 0,\n  y: 0,\n  parent: null as Entity | null, // \u274c Bad\n})\n```\n\n```typescript\n// Do this instead\nconst ChildOf = relation()\nconst child = world.spawn(Transform, ChildOf(parent))\nconst parent = child.targetFor(ChildOf) // \u2705 Good\n```\n\n### \u274c Using arrays to track children on the parent\n\n```typescript\n// Don\u0027t do this - manual bookkeeping, error-prone\nconst Parent = trait({\n  children: () =\u003e [] as Entity[], // \u274c Bad\n})\n```\n\n```typescript\n// Do this instead - query for children\nconst ChildOf = relation()\nconst children = world.query(ChildOf(parent)) // \u2705 Good\n```\n\n### \u274c Forgetting autoDestroy for hierarchies\n\n```typescript\n// Dangerous - orphans left behind when parent destroyed\nconst ChildOf = relation() // \u274c Missing autoDestroy\n```\n\n```typescript\n// Safe - children cleaned up automatically\nconst ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: \u0027orphan\u0027 }) // \u2705 Good\n```\n\n### \u274c Multiple relations when exclusive is needed\n\n```typescript\n// Bug-prone - entity can target multiple\nconst Targeting = relation()\nenemy.add(Targeting(playerA))\nenemy.add(Targeting(playerB)) // Now targeting both! \u274c\n```\n\n```typescript\n// Correct - only one target allowed\nconst Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true })\nenemy.add(Targeting(playerA))\nenemy.add(Targeting(playerB)) // Replaces playerA \u2705\n```\n\n### \u274c Querying without wildcard when you want all\n\n```typescript\n// This finds nothing - no specific target provided\nconst allChildren = world.query(ChildOf) // \u274c Wrong\n```\n\n```typescript\n// Use wildcard to query all entities with any target\nconst allChildren = world.query(ChildOf(\u0027*\u0027)) // \u2705 Correct\n```\n",

---
title: Change detection
description: Propagating and detecting changes
nav: 11

## `updateEach`

By default, `updateEach` will automatically turn on change detection for traits that are being tracked via `onChange` or the `Changed` modifier. If you want to silence change detection for a loop or force it to always run, you can do so with an options config.

```js
// Setting changeDetection to 'never' will silence it, triggering no change events
world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([position, velocity]) => {}, { changeDetection: 'never' })

// Setting changeDetection to 'always' will ignore selective tracking and always emit change events for all traits that are mutated
world
  .query(Position, Velocity)
  .updateEach(([position, velocity]) => {}, { changeDetection: 'always' })
```

Changed detection shallowly compares the scalar values just like React. This means objects and arrays will only be detected as changed if a new object or array is committed to the store. While immutable state is a great design pattern, it creates memory pressure and reduces performance so instead you can mutate and manually flag that a changed has occured.

```js
// ❌ This change will not be detected since the array is mutated and will pass the comparison
world.query(Inventory).updateEach(([inventory]) => {
  inventory.items.push(item)
})

// ✅ This change will be detected since a new array is created and the comparison will fail
world.query(Inventory).updateEach(([inventory]) => {
  inventory.items = [...inventory.items, item]
})

// ✅ This change is manually flagged and we still get to mutate for performance
world.query(Inventory).updateEach(([inventory], entity) => {
  inventory.items.push(item)
  entity.changed()
})
```

OUTPUT:
---
title: Query Modifiers
description: Using modifiers with queries
nav: 6
---

Modifiers are used to filter query results enabling powerful patterns. All modifiers can be mixed together.

## Not

The `Not` modifier excludes entities that have specific traits from the query results.

```js
import { Not } from 'koota'

const staticEntities = world.query(Position, Not(Velocity))
```

## Or

By default all query parameters are combined with logical AND. The `Or` modifier enables using logical OR instead.

```js
import { Or } from 'koota'

const movingOrVisible = world.query(Or(Velocity, Renderable))
```

## Added

The `Added` modifier tracks all entities that have added the specified traits or relations since the last time the query was run. A new instance of the modifier must be created for tracking to be unique.

When multiple traits are passed to `Added` it uses logical `AND`. Only entities where **all** specified traits have been added will be returned.

```js
import { createAdded } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()

// Track entities that added the Position trait
const newPositions = world.query(Added(Position))

// Track entities that added a ChildOf relation
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))

// Track entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were added
const fullyAdded = world.query(Added(Position, Velocity))

// Track entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was added
const eitherAdded = world.query(Or(Added(Position), Added(Velocity)))

// After running the query, the Added modifier is reset
```

## Removed

The `Removed` modifier tracks all entities that have removed the specified traits or relations since the last time the query was run. This includes entities that have been destroyed. A new instance of the modifier must be created for tracking to be unique.

When multiple traits are passed to `Removed` it uses logical `AND`. Only entities where **all** specified traits have been removed will be returned.

```js
import { createRemoved } from 'koota'

const Removed = createRemoved()

// Track entities that removed the Velocity trait
const stoppedEntities = world.query(Removed(Velocity))

// Track entities that removed a ChildOf relation
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))

// Track entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were removed
const fullyRemoved = world.query(Removed(Position, Velocity))

// Track entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was removed
const eitherRemoved = world.query(Or(Removed(Position), Removed(Velocity)))

// After running the query, the Removed modifier is reset
```

## Changed

The `Changed` modifier tracks all entities that have had the specified traits or relation stores change since the last time the query was run. A new instance of the modifier must be created for tracking to be unique.

When multiple traits are passed to `Changed` it uses logical `AND`. Only entities where **all** specified traits have changed will be returned.

```js
import { createChanged } from 'koota'

const Changed = createChanged()

// Track entities whose Position has changed
const movedEntities = world.query(Changed(Position))

// Track entities whose ChildOf relation data has changed
const updatedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))

// Track entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity have changed
const fullyUpdated = world.query(Changed(Position, Velocity))

// Track entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity has changed
const eitherChanged = world.query(Or(Changed(Position), Changed(Velocity)))

// After running the query, the Changed modifier is reset
```

## Add, remove and change events

Koota allows you to subscribe to add, remove, and change events for specific traits.

- `onAdd` triggers when `entity.add()` is called after the initial value has been set on the trait.
- `onRemove` triggers when `entity.remove()` is called, but before any data has been removed.
- `onChange` triggers when an entity's trait value has been set with `entity.set()` or when it is manually flagged with `entity.changed()`.

```js
// Subscribe to Position changes
const unsub = world.onChange(Position, (entity) => {
  console.log(`Entity ${entity} changed position`)
})

// Subscribe to Position additions
const unsub = world.onAdd(Position, (entity) => {
  console.log(`Entity ${entity} added position`)
})

// Subscribe to Position removals
const unsub = world.onRemove(Position, (entity) => {
  console.log(`Entity ${entity} removed position`)
})

// Trigger events
const entity = world.spawn(Position)
entity.set(Position, { x: 10, y: 20 })
entity.remove(Position)
```

When subscribing to relations, callbacks receive `(entity, target)` so you know which relation pair changed. Relation `onChange` events are triggered by `entity.set(Relation(target), data)` and only on relations with data via the store prop.

```js
const Likes = relation()

const unsub = world.onAdd(Likes, (entity, target) => {
  console.log(`Entity ${entity} likes ${target}`)
})
```

import { Brand } from '../common';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
    } as const;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isModifier(param: QueryParameter): param is Modifier {
    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;
}

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

/** Get the tracking type from a modifier */
export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

/** Check if an Or modifier has nested modifiers */
export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import type { Modifier, OrModifier, OrParameter } from '../types';
import { $modifier, createModifier } from '../modifier';

export const Or = <T extends OrParameter[]>(...params: T): OrModifier<T> => {
    // Separate traits from nested modifiers
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const modifiers: Modifier[] = [];

    for (const param of params) {
        if ((param as Modifier)[$modifier]) {
            modifiers.push(param as Modifier);
        } else {
            traits.push(param as Trait);
        }
    }

    const modifier = createModifier('or', 2, traits) as OrModifier<T>;
    modifier.modifiers = modifiers;

    return modifier;
};
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { createModifier } from '../modifier';

export const Not = <T extends Trait[] = Trait[]>(...traits: T): Modifier<T, 'not'> => {
    return createModifier('not', 1, traits);
};

182 packages/core/src/query/types.ts
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import type { RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { AoSFactory } from '../storage';
import type {
    ExtractSchema,
    ExtractStore,
    IsTag,
    Trait,
    TraitInstance,
    TraitRecord,
} from '../trait/types';
import type { SparseSet } from '../utils/sparse-set';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { $modifier } from './modifier';
import { $parameters, $queryRef } from './symbols';

export type QueryModifier = (...components: Trait[]) => Modifier;
export type QueryParameter = Trait | RelationPair | ReturnType<QueryModifier>;
export type QuerySubscriber = (entity: Entity) => void;
export type QueryUnsubscriber = () => void;

export type QueryResultOptions = {
    changeDetection?: 'always' | 'auto' | 'never';
};

export type QueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[] = QueryParameter[]> = readonly Entity[] & {
    readEach: (
        callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
    ) => QueryResult<T>;
    updateEach: (
        callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
        options?: QueryResultOptions
    ) => QueryResult<T>;
    useStores: (
        callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void
    ) => QueryResult<T>;
    select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U>;
    sort(callback?: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number): QueryResult<T>;
};

type UnwrapModifierData<T> = T extends Modifier<infer C> ? C : never;

export type StoresFromParameters<T extends QueryParameter[]> = T extends [infer First, ...infer Rest]
    ? [
          ...(First extends Trait
              ? [ExtractStore<First>]
              : First extends Modifier
                ? StoresFromParameters<UnwrapModifierData<First>>
                : []),
          ...(Rest extends QueryParameter[] ? StoresFromParameters<Rest> : []),
      ]
    : [];

export type InstancesFromParameters<T extends QueryParameter[]> = T extends [
    infer First,
    ...infer Rest,
]
    ? [
          ...(First extends Trait
              ? IsTag<First> extends false
                  ? ExtractSchema<First> extends AoSFactory
                      ? [ReturnType<ExtractSchema<First>>]
                      : [TraitRecord<First>]
                  : []
              : First extends Modifier
                ? IsNotModifier<First> extends true
                    ? []
                    : InstancesFromParameters<UnwrapModifierData<First>>
                : []),
          ...(Rest extends QueryParameter[] ? InstancesFromParameters<Rest> : []),
      ]
    : [];

export type IsNotModifier<T> =
    T extends Modifier<Trait[], infer TType> ? (TType extends 'not' ? true : false) : false;

export type QueryHash = string;

export type Query<T extends QueryParameter[] = QueryParameter[]> = {
    readonly [$queryRef]: true;
    /** Public read-only ID for fast array lookups */
    readonly id: number;
    /** Hash string for deduplication */
    readonly hash: QueryHash;
    /** Query parameters for creating instances */
    readonly parameters: T;
    readonly [$parameters]: T;
};

export type Modifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string> = {
    [$modifier]: true;
    type: TType;
    id: number;
    traits: TTrait;
    traitIds: number[];
};

/** Parameter types that can be passed to Or modifier */
export type OrParameter = Trait | Modifier;

/** Or modifier that can contain both traits and nested modifiers */
export type OrModifier<T extends OrParameter[] = OrParameter[]> = Modifier<
    ExtractTraitsFromOrParams<T>,
    'or'
> & {
    modifiers: Modifier[];
};

/** Extract traits from Or parameters (filters out modifiers) */
type ExtractTraitsFromOrParams<T extends OrParameter[]> = T extends [infer First, ...infer Rest]
    ? First extends Trait
        ? Rest extends OrParameter[]
            ? [First, ...ExtractTraitsFromOrParams<Rest>]
            : [First]
        : Rest extends OrParameter[]
          ? ExtractTraitsFromOrParams<Rest>
          : []
    : [];

/**
 * Unified tracking group that supports both AND and OR logic.
 * Replaces the old separate tracking arrays and OrTrackingGroup.
 */
export type TrackingGroup = {
    /** Whether all traits must match (and) or any trait can match (or) */
    logic: 'and' | 'or';
    /** The type of tracking event */
    type: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change';
    /** Tracking modifier ID for snapshot/mask lookups */
    id: number;
    /** Bitmasks indexed by generationId */
    bitmasks: (number | undefined)[];
    /** Per-entity tracker state indexed by [generationId][entityId] */
    trackers: (number[] | undefined)[];
};

export type QueryInstance<T extends QueryParameter[] = QueryParameter[]> = {
    version: number;
    world: World;
    parameters: T;
    hash: QueryHash;
    traits: Trait[];
    /** Static trait instances for non-tracking query matching */
    traitInstances: {
        required: TraitInstance[];
        forbidden: TraitInstance[];
        or: TraitInstance[];
        all: TraitInstance[];
    };
    /** Static bitmasks for non-tracking query matching (indexed by generationId) */
    staticBitmasks: {
        required: number;
        forbidden: number;
        or: number;
    }[];
    /** Unified tracking groups with explicit AND/OR logic */
    trackingGroups: TrackingGroup[];
    generations: number[];
    entities: SparseSet;
    isTracking: boolean;
    hasChangedModifiers: boolean;
    changedTraits: Set<Trait>;
    toRemove: SparseSet;
    addSubscriptions: Set<QuerySubscriber>;
    removeSubscriptions: Set<QuerySubscriber>;
    /** Relation pairs for target-specific queries */
    relationFilters?: RelationPair[];
    run: (world: World, params: QueryParameter[]) => QueryResult<T>;
    add: (entity: Entity) => void;
    remove: (world: World, entity: Entity) => void;
    check: (world: World, entity: Entity) => boolean;
    checkTracking: (
        world: World,
        entity: Entity,
        eventType: 'add' | 'remove' | 'change',
        generationId: number,
        bitflag: number
    ) => boolean;
    resetTrackingBitmasks: (eid: number) => void;
};

export type EventType = 'add' | 'remove' | 'change';

    33	export function runQuery<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    34	    world: World,
    35	    query: QueryInstance<T>,
    36	    params: QueryParameter[]
    37	): QueryResult<T> {
    38	    commitQueryRemovals(world);
    39	
    40	    // With hybrid bitmask strategy, query.entities is already incrementally maintained
    41	    // with both trait and relation filters applied. Just return the pre-filtered entities.
    42	    const entities = query.entities.dense.slice() as Entity[];
    43	
    44	    // Clear so it can accumulate again.
    45	    if (query.isTracking) {
    46	        query.entities.clear();
    47	        // PERF: Use indexed loop instead of for...of
    48	        const len = entities.length;
    49	        for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    50	            query.resetTrackingBitmasks(entities[i]);
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    return createQueryResult(world, entities, query, params);
    55	}

    57	export function addEntityToQuery(query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity) {
    58	    query.toRemove.remove(entity);
    59	    query.entities.add(entity);
    60	
    61	    // Notify subscriptions.
    62	    for (const sub of query.addSubscriptions) {
    63	        sub(entity);
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    query.version++;
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function removeEntityFromQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity) {
    70	    if (!query.entities.has(entity) || query.toRemove.has(entity)) return;
    71	
    72	    const ctx = world[$internal];
    73	
    74	    query.toRemove.add(entity);
    75	    ctx.dirtyQueries.add(query);
    76	
    77	    // Notify subscriptions.
    78	    for (const sub of query.removeSubscriptions) {
    79	        sub(entity);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    query.version++;
    83	}
    84	
    85	export function commitQueryRemovals(world: World) {
    86	    const ctx = world[$internal];
    87	    if (!ctx.dirtyQueries.size) return;
    88	
    89	    for (const query of ctx.dirtyQueries) {
    90	        for (let i = query.toRemove.dense.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    91	            const eid = query.toRemove.dense[i];
    92	            query.toRemove.remove(eid);
    93	            query.entities.remove(eid);
    94	        }
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    ctx.dirtyQueries.clear();
    98	}

   100	/** Reset tracking state for an entity across all tracking groups */
   101	export function resetQueryTrackingBitmasks(query: QueryInstance, eid: number) {
   102	    const groups = query.trackingGroups;
   103	    const len = groups.length;
   104	    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   105	        const trackers = groups[i].trackers;
   106	        const trackersLen = trackers.length;
   107	        for (let j = 0; j < trackersLen; j++) {
   108	            const tracker = trackers[j];
   109	            if (tracker) tracker[eid] = 0;
   110	        }
   111	    }
   112	}

   114	/**
   115	 * Unified function to process tracking modifiers with explicit AND/OR logic.
   116	 * Groups modifiers by (type, id, logic) key so same-tracker calls are combined.
   117	 */
   118	function processTrackingModifier(
   119	    world: World,
   120	    query: QueryInstance,
   121	    modifier: Modifier,
   122	    logic: 'and' | 'or',
   123	    ctx: World[typeof $internal],
   124	    groupsMap: Map<string, TrackingGroup>
   125	): void {
   126	    const trackingType = getTrackingType(modifier);
   127	    if (!trackingType) return;
   128	
   129	    const id = modifier.id;
   130	    // Key includes logic so Changed(A) at top-level stays separate from Or(Changed(A))
   131	    const key = `${trackingType}-${id}-${logic}`;
   132	
   133	    // Find or create tracking group
   134	    let group = groupsMap.get(key);
   135	    if (!group) {
   136	        group = {
   137	            logic,
   138	            type: trackingType,
   139	            id,
   140	            bitmasks: [],
   141	            trackers: [],
   142	        };
   143	        groupsMap.set(key, group);
   144	        query.trackingGroups.push(group);
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    // Register traits and build bitmasks
   148	    for (const trait of modifier.traits) {
   149	        if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
   150	        const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;
   151	        query.traits.push(trait);
   152	
   153	        // Add to traitInstances.all for query registration
   154	        query.traitInstances.all.push(instance);
   155	
   156	        // Build bitmasks by generation
   157	        const genId = instance.generationId;
   158	        group.bitmasks[genId] = (group.bitmasks[genId] || 0) | instance.bitflag;
   159	
   160	        // Track changed traits for change detection in query-result
   161	        if (trackingType === 'change') {
   162	            query.changedTraits.add(trait);
   163	            query.hasChangedModifiers = true;
   164	        }
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    query.isTracking = true;
   168	}

   219	        const parameter = parameters[i];

     3	import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
     4	import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
     5	import type { Relation } from '../relation/types';
     6	import { Store } from '../storage';
     7	import { getStore } from '../trait/trait';
     8	import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
     9	import { shallowEqual } from '../utils/shallow-equal';
    10	import type { World } from '../world';
    11	import { isModifier } from './modifier';
    12	import { setChanged } from './modifiers/changed';
    13	import type {
    14	    InstancesFromParameters,
    15	    QueryInstance,
    16	    QueryParameter,
    17	    QueryResult,
    18	    QueryResultOptions,
    19	    StoresFromParameters,
    20	} from './types';
    21	
    22	export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    23	    world: World,
    24	    entities: Entity[],
    25	    query: QueryInstance,
    26	    params: QueryParameter[]
    27	): QueryResult<T> {
    28	    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    29	    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];
    30	
    31	    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);
    32	
    33	    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
    34	        readEach(
    35	            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
    36	        ) {
    37	            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length }) as InstancesFromParameters<T>;
    38	
    39	            for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
    40	                const entity = entities[i];
    41	                const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    42	
    43	                // Create snapshots without atomic tracking
    44	                createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state);
    45	
    46	                callback(state, entity, i);
    47	            }
    48	
    49	            return results;
    50	        },
    51	
    52	        updateEach(
    53	            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
    54	            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
    55	        ) {
    56	            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });
    57	
    58	            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
    59	            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
    60	                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
    61	                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
    62	                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
    63	                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];
    64	
    65	                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);
    66	
    67	                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
    68	                    const entity = entities[i];
    69	                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    70	
    71	                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
    72	                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);
    73	
    74	                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
    75	                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;
    76	
    77	                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
    78	                    for (let j = 0; j < trackedIndices.length; j++) {
    79	                        const index = trackedIndices[j];
    80	                        const trait = traits[index];
    81	                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
    82	                        const newValue = state[index];
    83	                        const store = stores[index];
    84	
    85	                        let changed = false;
    86	                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
    87	                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
    88	                            if (!changed) {
    89	                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[index]);
    90	                            }
    91	                        } else {
    92	                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
    93	                        }
    94	
    95	                        // Collect changed traits.
    96	                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
    97	                    }
    98	
    99	                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for untracked traits.
   100	                    for (let j = 0; j < untrackedIndices.length; j++) {
   101	                        const index = untrackedIndices[j];
   102	                        const trait = traits[index];
   103	                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
   104	                        const store = stores[index];
   105	                        ctx.fastSet(eid, store, state[index]);
   106	                    }
   107	                }
   108	
   109	                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
   110	                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
   111	                    const [entity, trait] = changedPairs[i];
   112	                    setChanged(world, entity, trait);
   113	                }
   114	            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'always') {
   115	                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
   116	                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
   117	
   118	                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
   119	                    const entity = entities[i];
   120	                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
   121	
   122	                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
   123	                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);
   124	
   125	                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
   126	                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;
   127	
   128	                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
   129	                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
   130	                        const trait = traits[j];
   131	                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
   132	                        const newValue = state[j];
   133	
   134	                        let changed = false;
   135	                        if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
   136	                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
   137	                            if (!changed) {
   138	                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[j]);
   139	                            }
   140	                        } else {
   141	                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
   142	                        }
   143	
   144	                        // Collect changed traits.
   145	                        if (changed) changedPairs.push([entity, trait] as const);
   146	                    }
   147	                }
   148	
   149	                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
   150	                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
   151	                    const [entity, trait] = changedPairs[i];
   152	                    setChanged(world, entity, trait);
   153	                }
   154	            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'never') {
   155	                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
   156	                    const entity = entities[i];
   157	                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
   158	                    createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state);
   159	                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);
   160	
   161	                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
   162	                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;
   163	
   164	                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
   165	                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
   166	                        const trait = traits[j];
   167	                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
   168	                        ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
   169	                    }
   170	                }
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            return results;
   174	        },
   175	
   176	        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
   177	            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
   178	            return results;
   179	        },
   180	
   181	        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
   182	            traits.length = 0;
   183	            stores.length = 0;
   184	            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);
   185	            return results as unknown as QueryResult<U>;
   186	        },
   187	
   188	        sort(
   189	            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
   190	        ): QueryResult<T> {
   191	            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
   192	            return results;
   193	        },
   194	    });
   195	
   196	    return results;
   197	}

   200	    traits: Trait[],

     1	import { $internal } from '../common';
     2	import { getEntitiesWithRelationTo, getRelationTargets } from '../relation/relation';
     3	import { addTrait, cleanupRelationTarget, removeTrait } from '../trait/trait';
     4	import type { ConfigurableTrait } from '../trait/types';
     5	import { universe } from '../universe/universe';
     6	import type { World } from '../world';
     7	import type { Entity } from './types';
     8	import { allocateEntity, releaseEntity } from './utils/entity-index';
     9	import { getEntityId, getEntityWorldId } from './utils/pack-entity';
    10	
    11	// Ensure entity methods are patched.
    12	import './entity-methods-patch';
    13	
    14	export function createEntity(world: World, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]): Entity {
    15	    const ctx = world[$internal];
    16	    const entity = allocateEntity(ctx.entityIndex);
    17	
    18	    for (const query of ctx.notQueries) {
    19	        const match = query.check(world, entity);
    20	        if (match) query.add(entity);
    21	        // Reset all tracking bitmasks for the query.
    22	        query.resetTrackingBitmasks(getEntityId(entity));
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    ctx.entityTraits.set(entity, new Set());
    26	    addTrait(world, entity, ...traits);
    27	
    28	    return entity;
    29	}
    30	
    31	const cachedSet = new Set<Entity>();
    32	const cachedQueue = [] as Entity[];
    33	
    34	export function destroyEntity(world: World, entity: Entity) {
    35	    const ctx = world[$internal];
    36	
    37	    // Check if entity exists.
    38	    if (!world.has(entity)) throw new Error('Koota: The entity being destroyed does not exist.');
    39	
    40	    // Caching the lookup in the outer scope of the loop increases performance.
    41	    const entityQueue = cachedQueue;
    42	    const processedEntities = cachedSet;
    43	
    44	    // Ensure the queue is empty before starting.
    45	    entityQueue.length = 0;
    46	    entityQueue.push(entity);
    47	    processedEntities.clear();
    48	
    49	    // Destroyed entities may be the target or source of relations.
    50	    // To avoid stale references, all these relations must be removed.
    51	    // autoDestroy controls cascade behavior:
    52	    // - 'source' (or 'orphan'): when target dies, destroy sources (e.g., parent dies → children die)
    53	    // - 'target': when source dies, destroy targets (e.g., container dies → items die)
    54	    while (entityQueue.length > 0) {
    55	        const currentEntity = entityQueue.pop()!;
    56	        if (processedEntities.has(currentEntity)) continue;
    57	
    58	        processedEntities.add(currentEntity);
    59	
    60	        for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
    61	            const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
    62	
    63	            // Handle entities that have relations pointing TO currentEntity (currentEntity is target)
    64	            // If autoDestroy is 'orphan', destroy those sources
    65	            const sources = getEntitiesWithRelationTo(world, relation, currentEntity);
    66	            for (const source of sources) {
    67	                if (!world.has(source)) continue;
    68	
    69	                // Remove the relation from source to currentEntity
    70	                cleanupRelationTarget(world, relation, source, currentEntity);
    71	
    72	                // If autoDestroy: 'source', queue the source for destruction
    73	                if (relationCtx.autoDestroy === 'source') entityQueue.push(source);
    74	            }
    75	
    76	            // Handle relations where currentEntity is the source pointing to targets
    77	            // If autoDestroy is 'target', destroy those targets
    78	            if (relationCtx.autoDestroy === 'target') {
    79	                const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, currentEntity);
    80	                for (const target of targets) {
    81	                    if (!world.has(target)) continue;
    82	                    if (!processedEntities.has(target)) entityQueue.push(target);
    83	                }
    84	            }
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        // Remove all traits of the current entity.
    88	        const entityTraits = ctx.entityTraits.get(currentEntity);
    89	        if (entityTraits) {
    90	            for (const trait of entityTraits) {
    91	                removeTrait(world, currentEntity, trait);
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	
    95	        // Free the entity.
    96	        releaseEntity(ctx.entityIndex, currentEntity);
    97	
    98	        // Remove the entity from the all query.
    99	        const allQuery = ctx.queriesHashMap.get('');
   100	        if (allQuery) allQuery.remove(world, currentEntity);
   101	
   102	        // Remove all entity state from world.
   103	        ctx.entityTraits.delete(currentEntity);
   104	
   105	        // Clear entity bitmasks.
   106	        const eid = getEntityId(currentEntity);
   107	        for (let i = 0; i < ctx.entityMasks.length; i++) {
   108	            ctx.entityMasks[i][eid] = 0;
   109	        }
   110	    }
   111	}
   112	
   113	/* @inline @pure */ export function getEntityWorld(entity: Entity) {
   114	    const worldId = getEntityWorldId(entity);
   115	    return universe.worlds[worldId]!;
   116	}

import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import type {
    ConfigurableTrait,
    ExtractSchema,
    SetTraitCallback,
    Trait,
    TraitRecord,
    TraitValue,
} from '../trait/types';

export type Entity = number & {
    add: (...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) => void;
    remove: (...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) => void;
    has: (trait: Trait | RelationPair) => boolean;
    destroy: () => void;
    changed: (trait: Trait) => void;
    set: <T extends Trait | RelationPair>(
        trait: T,
        value: TraitValue<ExtractSchema<T>> | SetTraitCallback<T>,
        flagChanged?: boolean
    ) => void;
    get: <T extends Trait | RelationPair>(trait: T) => TraitRecord<ExtractSchema<T>> | undefined;
    targetFor: <T extends Trait>(relation: Relation<T>) => Entity | undefined;
    targetsFor: <T extends Trait>(relation: Relation<T>) => Entity[];
    id: () => number;
    generation: () => number;
    isAlive: () => boolean;
};

import { Brand } from '../../common';
import { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { Relation, RelationPair } from '../types';
import { $relation, $relationPair } from '../symbols';

/**
 * Check if a value is a Relation
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function isRelation(value: unknown): value is Relation<Trait> {
    return (value as Brand<typeof $relation> | null | undefined)?.[$relation] as unknown as boolean;
}

/**
 * Check if a value is a RelationPair
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function isRelationPair(value: unknown): value is RelationPair {
    return (value as Brand<typeof $relationPair> | null | undefined)?.[
        $relationPair
    ] as unknown as boolean;
}

   132	export function addTrait(world: World, entity: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
   133	    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
   134	        const config = traits[i];
   135	
   136	        // Handle relation pairs
   137	        if (isRelationPair(config)) {
   138	            addRelationPair(world, entity, config);
   139	            continue;
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        // Get trait and params for regular traits
   143	        let trait: Trait;
   144	        let params: Record<string, any> | undefined;
   145	
   146	        if (Array.isArray(config)) {
   147	            [trait, params] = config as [Trait, Record<string, any>];
   148	        } else {
   149	            trait = config as Trait;
   150	        }
   151	
   152	        // Add the trait to the entity
   153	        const data = addTraitToEntity(world, entity, trait);
   154	        if (!data) continue; // Already had the trait
   155	
   156	        // Initialize values
   157	        const traitCtx = trait[$internal];
   158	
   159	        const defaults = isOrderedTrait(trait)
   160	            ? getOrderedTrait(world, entity, trait)
   161	            : getSchemaDefaults(data.schema, traitCtx.type);
   162	
   163	        if (traitCtx.type === 'aos') {
   164	            setTrait(world, entity, trait, params ?? defaults, false);
   165	        } else if (defaults) {
   166	            setTrait(world, entity, trait, { ...defaults, ...params }, false);
   167	        } else if (params) {
   168	            setTrait(world, entity, trait, params, false);
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        // Call add subscriptions after values are set
   172	        for (const sub of data.addSubscriptions) sub(entity);
   173	    }
   174	}

   176	/**
   177	 * Add a relation pair to an entity.
   178	 */
   179	/* @inline */ function addRelationPair(world: World, entity: Entity, pair: RelationPair) {
   180	    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
   181	    const relation = pairCtx.relation;
   182	    const target = pairCtx.target;
   183	
   184	    // Only specific targets can be added (not wildcard '*')
   185	    if (typeof target !== 'number') return;
   186	
   187	    const params = pairCtx.params;
   188	    const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
   189	    const relationTrait = relationCtx.trait;
   190	
   191	    // Ignore if entity already relates to this target
   192	    // For example, adding Likes(alice) when this pair is already on the entity.
   193	    if (hasRelationToTarget(world, relation, entity, target)) return;
   194	
   195	    // For exclusive relations, remove the old target first
   196	    if (relationCtx.exclusive) {
   197	        const oldTarget = getFirstRelationTarget(world, relation, entity);
   198	        if (oldTarget !== undefined && oldTarget !== target) {
   199	            const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait);
   200	            if (instance) {
   201	                for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, oldTarget);
   202	            }
   203	            removeRelationTarget(world, relation, entity, oldTarget);
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    let instance = addTraitToEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   208	
   209	    const targetIndex = addRelationTarget(world, relation, entity, target);
   210	    if (targetIndex === -1) return; // No-op
   211	
   212	    const schema =
   213	        instance?.schema ?? getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait)!.schema;
   214	    const defaults = getSchemaDefaults(schema, relationTrait[$internal].type);
   215	
   216	    if (defaults) {
   217	        setRelationDataAtIndex(world, entity, relation, targetIndex, { ...defaults, ...params });
   218	    } else if (params) {
   219	        setRelationDataAtIndex(world, entity, relation, targetIndex, params);
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    // Fire add subscription for this pair
   223	    instance = instance ?? getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait)!;
   224	    for (const sub of instance.addSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
   225	}

   229	        const trait = traits[i];

   386	/* @inline */ function setTraitForPair(
   387	    world: World,
   388	    entity: Entity,
   389	    pair: RelationPair,
   390	    value: any,
   391	    triggerChanged: boolean
   392	) {

   395	    const target = pairCtx.target;

   406	/* @inline */ function setTraitForTrait(
   407	    world: World,
   408	    entity: Entity,
   409	    trait: Trait,
   410	    value: any,
   411	    triggerChanged: boolean
   412	) {
   413	    const ctx = trait[$internal];
   414	    const store = getStore(world, trait);
   415	    const index = getEntityId(entity);

   417	    // A short circuit is more performance than an if statement which creates a new code statement.
   418	    value instanceof Function && (value = value(ctx.get(index, store)));

   421	    triggerChanged && setChanged(world, entity, trait);

    37	    type HookInput = Trait | Relation<Trait> | RelationPair<Trait>;
    38	    type HookCallback = (entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void;
    39	
    40	    function resolveHookTrait(input: HookInput): Trait {
    41	        if (isRelationPair(input)) return input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait;
    42	        if (isRelation(input)) return input[$internal].trait;
    43	        return input;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    function resolveHookCallback(input: HookInput, callback: HookCallback): HookCallback {
    47	        if (isRelationPair(input)) {
    48	            const pairTarget = input[$internal].target;
    49	            if (pairTarget === '*') return callback;
    50	            return (entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => {
    51	                if (target === pairTarget) callback(entity, target);
    52	            };
    53	        }
    54	        return callback;
    55	    }

   101	            }
   102	            // Create world entity.
   103	            ctx.worldEntity = createEntity(world, IsExcluded, ...initTraits);
   104	        },

   109	
   110	        has(target: Entity | Trait): boolean {
   111	            return typeof target === 'number'

   314	        onAdd<T extends Trait>(
   315	            trait: T | Relation<T> | RelationPair<T>,
   316	            callback: (entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void
   317	        ): QueryUnsubscriber {
   318	            const ctx = world[$internal];
   319	            const resolvedTrait = resolveHookTrait(trait);
   320	            const resolvedCallback = resolveHookCallback(trait, callback);
   321	
   322	            let data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, resolvedTrait);
   323	
   324	            if (!data) {
   325	                registerTrait(world, resolvedTrait);
   326	                data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, resolvedTrait)!;
   327	            }
   328	
   329	            data.addSubscriptions.add(resolvedCallback);
   330	
   331	            return () => data.addSubscriptions.delete(resolvedCallback);
   332	        },

   335	            trait: T | Relation<T> | RelationPair<T>,

   337	        ): QueryUnsubscriber {
   338	            const ctx = world[$internal];
   339	            const resolvedTrait = resolveHookTrait(trait);
   340	            const resolvedCallback = resolveHookCallback(trait, callback);
   341	
   342	            let data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, resolvedTrait);

   354	        onChange(
   355	            trait: Trait | Relation<Trait> | RelationPair<Trait>,
   356	            callback: (entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void
   357	        ) {
   358	            const ctx = world[$internal];
   359	            const resolvedTrait = resolveHookTrait(trait);
   360	            const resolvedCallback = resolveHookCallback(trait, callback);
   361	
   362	            if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, resolvedTrait))
   363	                registerTrait(world, resolvedTrait);
   364	
   365	            const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, resolvedTrait)!;
   366	            data.changeSubscriptions.add(resolvedCallback);
   367	
   368	            ctx.trackedTraits.add(resolvedTrait);
   369	
   370	            return () => {
   371	                data.changeSubscriptions.delete(resolvedCallback);
   372	                if (data.changeSubscriptions.size === 0) ctx.trackedTraits.delete(resolvedTrait);
   373	            };
   374	        },

   392	    if (
   393	        optionsOrFirstTrait &&
   394	        typeof optionsOrFirstTrait === 'object' &&
   395	        !Array.isArray(optionsOrFirstTrait)
   396	    ) {
   397	        const { traits: optionTraits = [], lazy = false } = optionsOrFirstTrait as WorldOptions;
   398	        if (!lazy) {
   399	            world.init(...optionTraits);
   400	        } else {
   401	            lazyTraits = optionTraits;
   402	        }
   403	    } else {

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, relation, type TraitRecord, trait, universe } from '../src';

describe('World', () => {
    beforeEach(() => {
        universe.reset();
    });

    it('should create a world', () => {
        // World inits on creation.
        const world = createWorld();

        expect(world.isInitialized).toBe(true);
        expect(world.id).toBe(0);
        expect(universe.worlds[0]!).toBe(world);
        expect(universe.worldIndex.worldCursor).toBe(1);
    });

    it('should optionaly init lazily', () => {
        const world = createWorld({ lazy: true });
        expect(world.isInitialized).toBe(false);
        world.init();
        expect(world.isInitialized).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should reset the world', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        world.reset();

        // Always has one entity that is the world itself.
        expect(world.entities.length).toBe(1);
    });

    it('reset should remove entities with auto-destroy relations', () => {
        const Node = trait();
        const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan', exclusive: true });

        const world = createWorld();

        // Create a parent node and a child node.
        const parentNode = world.spawn(Node);
        world.spawn(Node, ChildOf(parentNode));

        // Expect this to not throw, since the ChildOf relation will automatically
        // remove the child node when the parent node is destroyed first.
        expect(() => world.reset()).not.toThrow();

        // Always has one entity that is the world itself.
        expect(world.entities.length).toBe(1);
    });

    it('destroy should lead to entities with auto-destroy relations being removed as well', () => {
        const Node = trait();
        const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'orphan', exclusive: true });

        const world = createWorld();

        // Create a parent node and two child nodes
        const parentNode = world.spawn(Node);
        world.spawn(Node, ChildOf(parentNode));
        world.spawn(Node, ChildOf(parentNode));

        // Expect this to not throw, since the ChildOf relation will automatically
        // remove the child node when the parent node is destroyed first
        expect(() => world.destroy()).not.toThrow();
    });

    it('errors if more than 16 worlds are created', () => {
        for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
            createWorld();
        }

        expect(() => createWorld()).toThrow();
    });

    it('should recycle world IDs when destroyed', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const id = world.id;

        world.destroy();

        const newWorld = createWorld();
        expect(newWorld.id).toBe(id);
    });

    it('should add, remove and get singletons', () => {
        const Test = trait({ last: 0, delta: 0 });

        const world = createWorld(Test);
        expect(world.has(Test)).toBe(true);

        const { last: then, delta } = world.get(Test)!;
        expect(then).toBe(0);
        expect(delta).toBe(0);

        const Time = trait({ last: 0, delta: 0 });

        world.add(Time);
        expect(world.has(Time)).toBe(true);
        expect(world.has(Test)).toBe(true);

        // Does not show up in a query.
        const query = world.query(Time);
        expect(query.length).toBe(0);

        const time = world.get(Time)!;
        time.last = 1;
        time.delta = 1;
        world.set(Time, time);

        expect(time.last).toBe(1);
        expect(time.delta).toBe(1);

        world.remove(Time);
        expect(world.has(Time)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should set singletons', () => {
        const Test = trait({ last: 0, delta: 0 });
        const world = createWorld(Test);

        world.set(Test, { last: 1, delta: 1 });

        expect(world.get(Test)!.last).toBe(1);
        expect(world.get(Test)!.delta).toBe(1);

        // Use callbacks to set.
        world.set(Test, (prev) => {
            return { last: prev.last + 1, delta: prev.delta + 1 };
        });

        expect(world.get(Test)!.last).toBe(2);
        expect(world.get(Test)!.delta).toBe(2);
    });

    it('should observe traits', () => {
        const TimeOfDay = trait({ hour: 0 });
        const world = createWorld(TimeOfDay);

        let timeOfDay: TraitRecord<typeof TimeOfDay> | undefined;
        world.onChange(TimeOfDay, (e) => {
            timeOfDay = e.get(TimeOfDay);
        });

        world.set(TimeOfDay, { hour: 1 });
        expect(timeOfDay).toEqual({ hour: 1 });
    });
});

     3	function createSoASetFunction(schema: Schema) {
     4	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
     5	
     6	    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
     7	    const setFunctionBody = keys
     8	        .map((key) => `if ('${key}' in value) store.${key}[index] = value.${key};`)
     9	        .join('\n    ');
    10	
    11	    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    12	    const set = new Function(
    13	        'index',
    14	        'store',
    15	        'value',
    16	        `
    17			${setFunctionBody}
    18		  `
    19	    );
    20	
    21	    return set;
    22	}

    43	// Return true if any trait value were changed.
    44	function createSoAFastSetChangeFunction(schema: Schema) {
    45	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
    46	
    47	    // Generate a hardcoded set function based on the schema keys
    48	    const setFunctionBody = keys
    49	        .map(
    50	            (key) =>
    51	                `if (store.${key}[index] !== value.${key}) {
    52	            store.${key}[index] = value.${key};
    53	            changed = true;
    54	        }`
    55	        )
    56	        .join('\n    ');
    57	
    58	    // Use new Function to create a set function with hardcoded keys
    59	    const set = new Function(
    60	        'index',
    61	        'store',
    62	        'value',
    63	        `
    64	        let changed = false;
    65	        ${setFunctionBody}
    66	        return changed;
    67	        `
    68	    );
    69	
    70	    return set;
    71	}
    72	
    73	function createSoAGetFunction(schema: Schema) {
    74	    const keys = Object.keys(schema);
    75	
    76	    // Create an object literal with all keys assigned from the store
    77	    const objectLiteral = `{ ${keys.map((key) => `${key}: store.${key}[index]`).join(', ')} }`;
    78	
    79	    // Use new Function to create a get function that returns the pre-populated object
    80	    const get = new Function(
    81	        'index',
    82	        'store',
    83	        `
    84	        return ${objectLiteral};
    85	        `
    86	    );
    87	
    88	    return get;
    89	}
    90	
    91	function createAoSSetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    92	    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
    93	        store[index] = value;
    94	    };
    95	}
    96	
    97	function createAoSFastSetChangeFunction(_schema: Schema) {
    98	    return (index: number, store: any, value: any) => {
    99	        let changed = false;
   100	        if (value !== store[index]) {
   101	            store[index] = value;
   102	            changed = true;
   103	        }
   104	        return changed;
   105	    };
   106	}
   107	
   108	function createAoSGetFunction(_schema: Schema) {
   109	    return (index: number, store: any) => store[index];
   110	}
   111	
   112	const noop = () => {};
   113	const createTagNoop = () => noop;

   131	};

   329	
   330	    // Add to notQueries if has forbidden traits
   331	    if (query.traitInstances.forbidden.length > 0) ctx.notQueries.add(query);
   332	
   333	    // Index queries with relation filters
   334	    const hasRelationFilters = query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0;
   335	
   336	    if (hasRelationFilters) {
   337	        for (const pair of query.relationFilters!) {
   338	            const relationTrait = pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait;
   339	            const relationTraitInstance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
   340	            if (relationTraitInstance) {
   341	                relationTraitInstance.relationQueries.add(query);
   342	            }
   343	        }
   344	    }
   345	
   346	    // Populate query with initial matching entities
   347	    if (query.trackingGroups.length > 0) {
   348	        // For tracking queries, check each entity against tracking groups
   349	        for (const group of query.trackingGroups) {
   350	            const { type, id, logic, bitmasks } = group;
   351	            const snapshot = ctx.trackingSnapshots.get(id)!;
   352	            const dirtyMask = ctx.dirtyMasks.get(id)!;
   353	            const changedMask = ctx.changedMasks.get(id)!;
   354	
   355	            for (const entity of ctx.entityIndex.dense) {
   356	                // For AND groups, skip if already in query (will be checked by other groups)
   357	                // For OR groups, skip if already in query
   358	                if (query.entities.has(entity)) continue;
   359	
   360	                const eid = getEntityId(entity);
   361	                let matches = logic === 'and'; // AND starts true, OR starts false
   362	
   363	                // Check each generation that has bitmasks
   364	                for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmasks.length; genId++) {
   365	                    const mask = bitmasks[genId];
   366	                    if (!mask) continue;
   367	
   368	                    const oldMask = snapshot[genId]?.[eid] || 0;
   369	                    const currentMask = ctx.entityMasks[genId]?.[eid] || 0;
   370	
   371	                    // Check each bit in the mask
   372	                    for (let bit = 1; bit <= mask; bit <<= 1) {
   373	                        if (!(mask & bit)) continue;
   374	
   375	                        let traitMatches = false;
   376	
   377	                        switch (type) {
   378	                            case 'add':
   379	                                traitMatches = (oldMask & bit) === 0 && (currentMask & bit) === bit;
   380	                                break;
   381	                            case 'remove':
   382	                                traitMatches =
   383	                                    ((oldMask & bit) === bit && (currentMask & bit) === 0) ||
   384	                                    ((oldMask & bit) === 0 &&
   385	                                        (currentMask & bit) === 0 &&
   386	                                        ((dirtyMask[genId]?.[eid] ?? 0) & bit) === bit);
   387	                                break;
   388	                            case 'change':
   389	                                traitMatches = ((changedMask[genId]?.[eid] ?? 0) & bit) === bit;
   390	                                break;
   391	                        }
   392	
   393	                        if (logic === 'and') {
   394	                            if (!traitMatches) {
   395	                                matches = false;
   396	                                break;
   397	                            }
   398	                        } else {
   399	                            // OR logic
   400	                            if (traitMatches) {
   401	                                matches = true;
   402	                                break;
   403	                            }
   404	                        }
   405	                    }
   406	
   407	                    // Early exit for AND that failed or OR that succeeded
   408	                    if (logic === 'and' && !matches) break;
   409	                    if (logic === 'or' && matches) break;
   410	                }
   411	
   412	                if (matches) {
   413	                    if (hasRelationFilters) {
   414	                        let relationMatch = true;
   415	                        for (const pair of query.relationFilters!) {
   416	                            if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, pair)) {
   417	                                relationMatch = false;
   418	                                break;
   419	                            }
   420	                        }
   421	                        if (relationMatch) query.add(entity);
   422	                    } else {
   423	                        query.add(entity);
   424	                    }
   425	                }
   426	            }
   427	        }
   428	    } else {
   429	        // Non-tracking query: populate immediately
   430	        const entities = ctx.entityIndex.dense;
   431	        for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
   432	            const entity = entities[i];
   433	            const match = hasRelationFilters
   434	                ? checkQueryWithRelations(world, query, entity)
   435	                : query.check(world, entity);
   436	            if (match) query.add(entity);
   437	        }
   438	    }
   439	
   440	    return query;
   441	}
   442	
   443	let queryId = 0;
   444	
   445	export function createQuery<T extends QueryParameter[]>(...parameters: T): Query<T> {

export const $internal = Symbol.for('koota.internal');

/**
 * Type utility for symbol-branded runtime type checks.
 * Allows accessing a symbol property while maintaining type safety.
 */
export type Brand<S extends symbol> = { readonly [K in S]?: true };
---
import type { Entity } from '../types';

// Constants for bit sizes
export const WORLD_ID_BITS = 4; // 4 bits can represent 0-15
export const GENERATION_BITS = 8; // 8 bits can represent 0-255
export const ENTITY_ID_BITS = 20; // 20 bits can represent 0-1,048,575
// Total bits used: 4 + 8 + 20 = 32 bits

// Masks for extracting values
export const WORLD_ID_MASK = (1 << WORLD_ID_BITS) - 1;
export const GENERATION_MASK = (1 << GENERATION_BITS) - 1;
export const ENTITY_ID_MASK = (1 << ENTITY_ID_BITS) - 1;

// Bit shifts for positioning each component
export const GENERATION_SHIFT = ENTITY_ID_BITS;
export const WORLD_ID_SHIFT = GENERATION_SHIFT + GENERATION_BITS;

export function packEntity(worldId: number, generation: number, entityId: number): Entity {
    return (((worldId & WORLD_ID_MASK) << WORLD_ID_SHIFT) |
        ((generation & GENERATION_MASK) << GENERATION_SHIFT) |
        (entityId & ENTITY_ID_MASK)) as Entity;
}

export function unpackEntity(entity: Entity) {
    return {
        worldId: entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
        generation: (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK,
        entityId: entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    };
}

export const getEntityId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK;
export const getEntityWorldId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT;
export const getEntityAndWorldId = /* @pure */ (entity: Entity): [number, number] => [
    entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
];

export const getEntityGeneration = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) =>
    (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK;

export const incrementGeneration = (entity: Entity): Entity =>
    ((entity & ~(GENERATION_MASK << GENERATION_SHIFT)) | // Clear current generation bits
        (((((entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK) + 1) & GENERATION_MASK) <<
            GENERATION_SHIFT)) as unknown as Entity; // Extract generation, increment, wrap around, shift back, and combine

import { Brand } from '../common';
import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,
    id: number,
    traits: TTrait
): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {
    return {
        [$modifier]: true,
        type,
        id,
        traits,
        traitIds: traits.map((trait) => trait.id),
    } as const;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function isModifier(param: QueryParameter): param is Modifier {
    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;
}

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

/** Get the tracking type from a modifier */
export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

/** Check if an Or modifier has nested modifiers */
export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createAdded() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelation[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `added-${number}`> => {
        const traits = inputs.map((input) =>
            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`added-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

import { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { EventType, Modifier, OrModifier, QueryParameter } from './types';

export const $modifier = Symbol('modifier');

export function createModifier<TTrait extends Trait[] = Trait[], TType extends string = string>(
    type: TType,

): Modifier<TTrait, TType> {

        type,

        traits,

    } as const;

    return (param as Brand<typeof $modifier> | null | undefined)?.[$modifier] as unknown as boolean;

/** Check if a modifier is a tracking modifier (added, removed, or changed) */
export function isTrackingModifier(modifier: Modifier): boolean {
    const { type } = modifier;
    return type.includes('added') || type.includes('removed') || type.includes('changed');
}

export function getTrackingType(modifier: Modifier): EventType | null {
    const { type } = modifier;
    if (type.includes('added')) return 'add';
    if (type.includes('removed')) return 'remove';
    if (type.includes('changed')) return 'change';
    return null;
}

export function isOrWithModifiers(modifier: Modifier): modifier is OrModifier {
    return modifier.type === 'or' && Array.isArray((modifier as OrModifier).modifiers);
}

/** Resolve tracking modifier inputs (traits, relations, relation pairs) into base traits + pairs. */
export function resolveTrackingInputs(
    inputs: (Trait | import('../relation/types').Relation<Trait> | RelationPair)[]
): { traits: Trait[]; pairs: RelationPair[] } {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const pairs: RelationPair[] = [];

    for (const input of inputs) {
        if (isRelationPair(input)) {
            pairs.push(input);
            traits.push(input[$internal].relation[$internal].trait);
        } else if (isRelation(input)) {
            traits.push(input[$internal].trait);
        } else {
            traits.push(input);
        }
    }

    return { traits, pairs };
}

            isRelation(input) ? input[$internal].trait : input
        ) as ExtractTraits<T>;
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}
