cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/pair-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../../relation/types';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, PairTrackerMap, PairTrackerState, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from './check-query-tracking-with-relations';

/**
 * Update (or create) the per-target pair tracker state for a pair event.
 */
export function updatePairTracker(
    map: PairTrackerMap,
    eid: number,
    relationTraitId: number,
    targetId: number,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): PairTrackerState {
    let relationMap = map.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) {
        relationMap = new Map();
        map.set(eid, relationMap);
    }
    let targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTraitId);
    if (!targetMap) {
        targetMap = new Map();
        relationMap.set(relationTraitId, targetMap);
    }
    let state = targetMap.get(targetId);
    if (!state) {
        state = { delta: 0, changed: false };
        targetMap.set(targetId, state);
    }

    if (eventType === 'add') state.delta++;
    else if (eventType === 'remove') state.delta--;
    else if (eventType === 'change') state.changed = true;

    if (data !== undefined) state.data = data;

    return state;
}

/**
 * Check whether a tracked relation pair has a matching event state for the given event type.
 * A wildcard target ('*') matches any target recorded for that relation trait.
 */
export function pairStateMatches(
    map: PairTrackerMap | undefined,
    eid: number,
    pair: RelationPair,
    eventType: EventType
): boolean {
    if (!map) return false;
    const relationMap = map.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) return false;

    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
    const relationTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
    const targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTrait.id);
    if (!targetMap) return false;

    const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;

    if (pairTarget === '*') {
        for (const state of targetMap.values()) {
            if (eventType === 'add' && state.delta > 0) return true;
            if (eventType === 'remove' && state.delta < 0) return true;
            if (eventType === 'change' && state.changed) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    if (typeof pairTarget !== 'number') return false;
    const state = targetMap.get(pairTarget);
    if (!state) return false;

    if (eventType === 'add' && state.delta > 0) return true;
    if (eventType === 'remove' && state.delta < 0) return true;
    if (eventType === 'change' && state.changed) return true;
    return false;
}

/**
 * Copy global pair events (since factory creation) into a query group's pair trackers.
 * Used when populating newly created query instances.
 */
export function seedPairTrackersFromGlobal(
    map: PairTrackerMap,
    globalEvents: PairTrackerMap | undefined,
    eid: number
): void {
    if (!globalEvents) return;
    const relationMap = globalEvents.get(eid);
    if (!relationMap) return;
    for (const [relationTraitId, targetMap] of relationMap) {
        for (const [targetId, state] of targetMap) {
            updatePairTracker(map, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, state.delta > 0 ? 'add' : state.delta < 0 ? 'remove' : state.changed ? 'change' : 'add', state.data);
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Log a pair event into the world-level tracking log so newly created query
 * instances can be populated. Also records the per-target data for iteration.
 */
export function logPairEvent(
    world: World,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    entity: Entity,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);

    for (const [id, events] of ctx.pairTrackingEvents) {
        void id;
        updatePairTracker(events, eid, relationTrait.id, targetId, eventType, data);
    }
}

/**
 * Notify all tracking queries that include a relation pair event.
 * Only queries with pair tracking are processed so trait-only queries do not
 * observe pair-level add/remove events (which would incorrectly match
 * non-first additions / non-last removals).
 */
export function notifyPairEvent(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relationTrait: Trait,
    target: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    data?: unknown
): void {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
    if (!instance) return;

    const { generationId, bitflag } = instance;

    // Log globally first so late-created queries can be populated.
    logPairEvent(world, relationTrait, entity, target, eventType, data);

    for (const query of instance.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasPairTracking) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      eventType,
                      generationId,
                      bitflag,
                      target
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, eventType, generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }
}

/**
 * Get the data for a relation pair, used by query result iteration. Prefers live
 * data; falls back to the last recorded event data (needed for Removed pairs).
 */
export function getPairTrackedData(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relation: Relation<Trait>,
    target: Entity
): unknown {
    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
    if (data !== undefined) return data;

    // Fall back to pair event state (global log) for removed pairs.
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);
    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;

    for (const events of ctx.pairTrackingEvents.values()) {
        const relationMap = events.get(eid);
        if (!relationMap) continue;
        const targetMap = relationMap.get(relationTrait.id);
        if (!targetMap) continue;
        const state = targetMap.get(targetId);
        if (state && state.data !== undefined) return state.data;
    }

    return undefined;
}

            const snapshot = ctx.trackingSnapshots.get(id)!;
            const dirtyMask = ctx.dirtyMasks.get(id)!;
            const changedMask = ctx.changedMasks.get(id)!;

                for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmasks.length; genId++) {

            const { type, id, logic, bitmasks } = group;
            const snapshot = ctx.trackingSnapshots.get(id)!;
            const dirtyMask = ctx.dirtyMasks.get(id)!;
            const changedMask = ctx.changedMasks.get(id)!;
            const globalPairEvents = ctx.pairTrackingEvents.get(id);

            for (const entity of ctx.entityIndex.dense) {
                // For AND groups, skip if already in query (will be checked by other groups)
                // For OR groups, skip if already in query
                if (query.entities.has(entity)) continue;

                const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                // Seed pair trackers from the global pair event log so events that
                // happened before this query instance was created are included.
                if (group.pairs.length > 0) {
                    seedPairTrackersFromGlobal(group.pairTrackers, globalPairEvents, eid);
                }

                let matches = logic === 'and'; // AND starts true, OR starts false

                // Check each generation that has bitmasks
                for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmasks.length; genId++) {
                    const mask = bitmasks[genId];
                    if (!mask) continue;

                    const oldMask = snapshot[genId]?.[eid] || 0;
                    const currentMask = ctx.entityMasks[genId]?.[eid] || 0;

                    // Check each bit in the mask
                    for (let bit = 1; bit <= mask; bit <<= 1) {
                        if (!(mask & bit)) continue;

                        let traitMatches = false;

                        switch (type) {
                            case 'add':
                                traitMatches = (oldMask & bit) === 0 && (currentMask & bit) === bit;
                                break;
                            case 'remove':
                                traitMatches =
                                    ((oldMask & bit) === bit && (currentMask & bit) === 0) ||
                                    ((oldMask & bit) === 0 &&
                                        (currentMask & bit) === 0 &&
                                        ((dirtyMask[genId]?.[eid] ?? 0) & bit) === bit);
                                break;
                            case 'change':
                                traitMatches = ((changedMask[genId]?.[eid] ?? 0) & bit) === bit;
                                break;
                        }

                        if (logic === 'and') {
                            if (!traitMatches) {
                                matches = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        } else {
                            // OR logic
                            if (traitMatches) {
                                matches = true;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Early exit for AND that failed or OR that succeeded
                    if (logic === 'and' && !matches) break;
                    if (logic === 'or' && matches) break;
                }

                // Check relation pair tracking state
                if (group.pairs.length > 0) {
                    const pairMatched = (() => {
                        if (logic === 'and') {
                            for (const pair of group.pairs) {
                                if (!pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, type)) {
                                    return false;
                                }
                            }
                            return true;
                        }
                        for (const pair of group.pairs) {
                            if (pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, type)) {
                                return true;
                            }
                        }
                        return false;
                    })();

                    if (logic === 'and') {
                        matches = matches && pairMatched;
                    } else {
                        matches = matches || pairMatched;
                    }
                }

                if (matches) {
                    if (hasRelationFilters) {
                        let relationMatch = true;
                        for (const pair of query.relationFilters!) {
                            if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, pair)) {
                                relationMatch = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (relationMatch) query.add(entity);
                    } else {
                        query.add(entity);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } else {"""

print("done")

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query-tracking.ts <<'EOF'
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import { World } from '../../world';
import { EventType, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { pairStateMatches, updatePairTracker } from './pair-tracking';

/**
 * Check if a group's relation-pair filters are affected by a pair event.
 * A wildcard pair target matches any target for the same relation trait.
 */
function groupAffectedByPairEvent(
    query: QueryInstance,
    group: QueryInstance['trackingGroups'][number],
    world: World,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target: Entity
): boolean {
    if (group.pairs.length === 0) return false;
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const targetId = getEntityId(target);
    const pairs = group.pairs;
    const len = pairs.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        const pair = pairs[i];
        const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
        const pairTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
        const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
        if (!instance) continue;
        if (instance.generationId === eventGenerationId && (instance.bitflag & eventBitflag)) {
            const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
            if (pairTarget === '*' || pairTarget === targetId) return true;
        }
    }
    void query;
    return false;
}

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a tracking query with event handling.
 *
 * PERF: This is a hot path - optimizations applied:
 * - Cache all property accesses at function start
 * - Use `| 0` instead of `|| 0` (bitwise coerces undefined to 0)
 * - Avoid optional chaining in inner loops
 * - Cache array references before mutation
 * - Early exits where possible
 */
export function checkQueryTracking(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target?: Entity
): boolean {
    // Cache all property accesses upfront
    const staticBitmasks = query.staticBitmasks;
    const trackingGroups = query.trackingGroups;
    const generations = query.generations;
    const traitInstancesAll = query.traitInstances.all;
    const entityMasks = world[$internal].entityMasks;
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    const generationsLen = generations.length;
    const trackingGroupsLen = trackingGroups.length;

    // Early exit: no traits to check
    if (traitInstancesAll.length === 0) return false;

    // 1. Check static constraints (required/forbidden/or)
    for (let i = 0; i < generationsLen; i++) {
        const generationId = generations[i];
        const bitmask = staticBitmasks[i];
        if (!bitmask) continue;

        const required = bitmask.required;
        const forbidden = bitmask.forbidden;
        const or = bitmask.or;

        // PERF: Direct access + bitwise OR coerces undefined to 0
        const genMasks = entityMasks[generationId];
        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;

        // Check forbidden traits
        if (forbidden && (entityMask & forbidden) !== 0) return false;

        // Check required traits
        if (required && (entityMask & required) !== required) return false;

        // Check Or traits
        if (or !== 0 && (entityMask & or) === 0) return false;
    }

    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // 2. Process tracking groups - update trackers and check cross-event invalidation
    // Also track OR group state to avoid second loop when possible
    let hasOrGroup = false;
    let anyOrMatched = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < trackingGroupsLen; i++) {
        const group = trackingGroups[i];
        const groupType = group.type;
        const groupLogic = group.logic;
        const groupBitmasks = group.bitmasks;
        const groupBitmask = groupBitmasks[eventGenerationId];
        const groupTrackers = group.trackers;
        const groupPairs = group.pairs;

        // Determine if this event affects the group's tracked traits
        const affectsTrait = groupBitmask && (groupBitmask & eventBitflag);

        if (affectsTrait) {
            let shouldProcess = true;

            // For pair groups, a pair event must match one of the group's pair
            // filters; otherwise it's a different target and must not affect this
            // group (per-target reactivity).
            if (groupPairs.length > 0 && target !== undefined) {
                if (!groupAffectedByPairEvent(query, group, world, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
                    shouldProcess = false;
                }
            }

            if (shouldProcess) {
                // Cross-event invalidation:
                // - Remove event invalidates Added/Changed tracking
                // - Add event invalidates Removed/Changed tracking
                if (eventType === 'remove') {
                    if (groupType === 'add' || groupType === 'change') return false;
                } else if (eventType === 'add') {
                    if (groupType === 'remove' || groupType === 'change') return false;
                }

                // Update tracker if event type matches group type
                if (groupType === eventType) {
                    // For change events, verify entity still has the trait
                    if (eventType === 'change') {
                        const genMasks = entityMasks[eventGenerationId];
                        const entityMask = genMasks ? (genMasks[eid] | 0) : 0;
                        if (!(entityMask & eventBitflag)) return false;
                    }

                    // PERF: Cache tracker array reference before mutation
                    let trackerArr = groupTrackers[eventGenerationId];
                    if (!trackerArr) {
                        trackerArr = [];
                        groupTrackers[eventGenerationId] = trackerArr;
                    }
                    trackerArr[eid] = (trackerArr[eid] | 0) | eventBitflag;
                }
            }
        }

        // Update pair trackers if this is a pair event matching the group
        if (target !== undefined && groupPairs.length > 0 && groupType === eventType) {
            if (groupAffectedByPairEvent(query, group, world, eventGenerationId, eventBitflag, target)) {
                const targetId = getEntityId(target);
                // Find the relation trait id from the first matching pair
                const pair = groupPairs.find((p) => {
                    const pairCtx = p[$internal];
                    const pairTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
                    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
                    if (!instance) return false;
                    if (instance.generationId !== eventGenerationId) return false;
                    if (!(instance.bitflag & eventBitflag)) return false;
                    const pairTarget = pairCtx.target;
                    return pairTarget === '*' || pairTarget === targetId;
                });
                if (pair) {
                    const relationTraitId = pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait.id;
                    updatePairTracker(group.pairTrackers, eid, relationTraitId, targetId, eventType);
                }
            }
        }

        // 3. Verify tracking group satisfaction (merged into same loop)
        if (groupLogic === 'or') {
            hasOrGroup = true;
            if (!anyOrMatched) {
                // Check if any trait in OR group has been tracked
                const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
                let traitMatched = false;
                for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                    const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                    if (!mask) continue;
                    const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
                    const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                    if (tracker & mask) {
                        traitMatched = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // Check if any pair in OR group has been tracked
                let pairMatched = false;
                if (groupPairs.length > 0) {
                    for (const pair of groupPairs) {
                        if (pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, groupType)) {
                            pairMatched = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (traitMatched || pairMatched) anyOrMatched = true;
            }
        } else {
            // AND group: all traits must be tracked
            const bitmaskLen = groupBitmasks.length;
            let traitMatched = true;
            for (let genId = 0; genId < bitmaskLen; genId++) {
                const mask = groupBitmasks[genId];
                if (!mask) continue;
                const trackerArr = groupTrackers[genId];
                const tracker = trackerArr ? (trackerArr[eid] | 0) : 0;
                if ((tracker & mask) !== mask) {
                    traitMatched = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            // AND group: all pairs must match
            let pairMatched = true;
            if (groupPairs.length > 0) {
                for (const pair of groupPairs) {
                    if (!pairStateMatches(group.pairTrackers, eid, pair, groupType)) {
                        pairMatched = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!traitMatched || !pairMatched) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    // If we have OR groups, at least one must match
    if (hasOrGroup && !anyOrMatched) {
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

cd /app && cat > packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations.ts <<'EOF'
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { hasRelationPair } from '../../relation/relation';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { EventType, QueryInstance } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTracking } from './check-query-tracking';

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a tracking query with relation filters.
 * Combines checkQueryTracking (trait bitmasks + tracking state) with relation checks.
 */
export function checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    entity: Entity,
    eventType: EventType,
    eventGenerationId: number,
    eventBitflag: number,
    target?: Entity
): boolean {
    // First check trait bitmasks and tracking state (fast)
    if (
        !checkQueryTracking(
            world,
            query,
            entity,
            eventType,
            eventGenerationId,
            eventBitflag,
            target
        )
    ) {
        return false;
    }

    // Then check relation pairs if any
    if (query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0) {
        for (const pair of query.relationFilters) {
            if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, pair)) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

   227	export function removeTrait(world: World, entity: Entity, ...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { getRelationData } from '../../relation/relation';
import { hasTrait, registerTrait } from '../../trait/trait';
import { getTraitInstance, hasTraitInstance } from '../../trait/trait-instance';
import type { ExtractTraits, Trait, TraitOrRelationOrPair } from '../../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import { createModifier, resolveTrackingInputs } from '../modifier';
import type { Modifier } from '../types';
import { checkQueryTrackingWithRelations } from '../utils/check-query-tracking-with-relations';
import { logPairEvent } from '../utils/pair-tracking';
import { createTrackingId, setTrackingMasks } from '../utils/tracking-cursor';

export function createChanged() {
    const id = createTrackingId();

    for (const world of universe.worlds) {
        if (!world) continue;
        setTrackingMasks(world, id);
    }

    return <T extends TraitOrRelationOrPair[]>(
        ...inputs: T
    ): Modifier<ExtractTraits<T>, `changed-${number}`> => {
        const { traits, pairs } = resolveTrackingInputs(inputs);
        return createModifier(`changed-${id}`, id, traits as ExtractTraits<T>, pairs);
    };
}

/** @inline */
function markChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target?: Entity) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Early exit if the trait is not on the entity.
    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;

    // Register the trait if it's not already registered.
    if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)) registerTrait(world, trait);
    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;

    // Mark the trait as changed in bitmasks for Changed modifiers.
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
    const { generationId, bitflag } = data;

    for (const changedMask of ctx.changedMasks.values()) {
        if (!changedMask[generationId]) changedMask[generationId] = [];
        if (!changedMask[generationId][eid]) changedMask[generationId][eid] = 0;
        changedMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
    }

    // Update tracking queries with change event
    for (const query of data.trackingQueries) {
        if (!query.hasChangedModifiers) continue;
        if (!query.changedTraits.has(trait)) continue;

        const match =
            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
                      world,
                      query,
                      entity,
                      'change',
                      generationId,
                      bitflag,
                      target
                  )
                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'change', generationId, bitflag, target);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        else query.remove(world, entity);
    }

    return data;
}

export function setChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait);
    if (!data) return;
    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity);
}

export function setPairChanged(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait, target: Entity) {
    const data = markChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
    if (!data) return;

    // Find the relation to capture per-target data for iteration.
    const relation = trait[$internal].relation;
    const pairData = relation ? getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target) : undefined;
    logPairEvent(world, trait, entity, target, 'change', pairData);

    for (const sub of data.changeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
}

import { $internal } from '../../common';
import { isRelationPair } from '../../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../../relation/types';
import type { Trait } from '../../trait/types';
import { isModifier } from '../modifier';
import type { QueryHash, QueryParameter } from '../types';

const sortedIDs = new Float64Array(1024); // Use Float64 for larger IDs with relation encoding

export const createQueryHash = (parameters: QueryParameter[]): QueryHash => {
    sortedIDs.fill(0);
    let cursor = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < parameters.length; i++) {
        const param = parameters[i];

        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            // Encode relation pair as: (relationTraitId * 1000000) + targetId
            // This ensures unique hashes for different relation/target combinations
            const pairCtx = param[$internal];
            const relation = pairCtx.relation;
            const target = pairCtx.target;

            const relationId = (relation as Relation<Trait>)[$internal].trait.id;
            const targetId = typeof target === 'number' ? target : -1;

            // Combine into a unique hash number
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = relationId * 10000000 + targetId + 5000000;
        } else if (isModifier(param)) {
            const modifierId = param.id;
            const traitIds = param.traitIds;

            for (let i = 0; i < traitIds.length; i++) {
                const traitId = traitIds[i];
                sortedIDs[cursor++] = modifierId * 100000 + traitId;
            }
        } else {
            const traitId = (param as Trait).id;
            sortedIDs[cursor++] = traitId;
        }
    }

    // Sort only the portion of the array that has been filled.
    const filledArray = sortedIDs.subarray(0, cursor);
    filledArray.sort();

    // Create string key.
    const hash = filledArray.join(',');

    return hash;
};

import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { setRelationData } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair, RelationTarget } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { Store } from '../storage';
import { getStore } from '../trait/trait';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { shallowEqual } from '../utils/shallow-equal';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { isModifier } from './modifier';
import { setChanged, setPairChanged } from './modifiers/changed';
import { getPairTrackedData } from './utils/pair-tracking';
import type {
    InstancesFromParameters,
    QueryInstance,
    QueryParameter,
    QueryResult,
    QueryResultOptions,
    StoresFromParameters,
} from './types';

type PairResolver = {
    relation: Relation<Trait>;
    target: Entity;
};

type ChangedPair = [Entity, Trait, Entity?];

export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    world: World,
    entities: Entity[],
    query: QueryInstance,
    params: QueryParameter[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];
    const pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[] = [];

    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, pairResolvers, world);

    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length }) as InstancesFromParameters<T>;

            for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                const entity = entities[i];

                // Create snapshots without atomic tracking
                createSnapshots(world, entity, traits, stores, pairResolvers, state);

                callback(state, entity, i);
            }

            return results;
        },

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                const changedPairs: ChangedPair[] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(
                        world,
                        entity,
                        traits,
                        stores,
                        pairResolvers,
                        state,
                        atomicSnapshots
                    );
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < trackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = trackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const resolver = pairResolvers[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[index];
                        const store = stores[index];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (resolver) {
                            changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            if (changed) {
                                setRelationData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target, newValue);
                            }
                        } else if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[index]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, store, newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) {
                            changedPairs.push(
                                resolver ? [entity, trait, resolver.target] : [entity, trait]
                            );
                        }
                    }

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores for untracked traits.
                    for (let j = 0; j < untrackedIndices.length; j++) {
                        const index = untrackedIndices[j];
                        const trait = traits[index];
                        const resolver = pairResolvers[index];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const store = stores[index];

                        if (resolver) {
                            setRelationData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target, state[index]);
                        } else {
                            ctx.fastSet(eid, store, state[index]);
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    else setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'always') {
                const changedPairs: ChangedPair[] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(
                        world,
                        entity,
                        traits,
                        stores,
                        pairResolvers,
                        state,
                        atomicSnapshots
                    );
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const resolver = pairResolvers[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        const newValue = state[j];

                        let changed = false;
                        if (resolver) {
                            changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[j]);
                            if (changed) {
                                setRelationData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target, newValue);
                            }
                        } else if (ctx.type === 'aos') {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                            if (!changed) {
                                changed = !shallowEqual(newValue, atomicSnapshots[j]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            changed = ctx.fastSetWithChangeDetection(eid, stores[j], newValue);
                        }

                        // Collect changed traits.
                        if (changed) {
                            changedPairs.push(
                                resolver ? [entity, trait, resolver.target] : [entity, trait]
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Trigger change events for each entity that was modified.
                for (let i = 0; i < changedPairs.length; i++) {
                    const [entity, trait, target] = changedPairs[i];
                    if (target !== undefined) setPairChanged(world, entity, trait, target);
                    else setChanged(world, entity, trait);
                }
            } else if (options.changeDetection === 'never') {
                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
                    createSnapshots(world, entity, traits, stores, pairResolvers, state);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const resolver = pairResolvers[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        if (resolver) {
                            setRelationData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target, state[j]);
                        } else {
                            ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },

        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
            traits.length = 0;
            stores.length = 0;
            pairResolvers.length = 0;
            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, pairResolvers, world);
            return results as unknown as QueryResult<U>;
        },

        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    });

    return results;
}

/* @inline */ function getTrackedTraits(
    traits: Trait[],
    world: World,
    query: QueryInstance,
    trackedIndices: number[],
    untrackedIndices: number[]
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const trait = traits[i];
        const hasTracked = world[$internal].trackedTraits.has(trait);
        const hasChanged = query.hasChangedModifiers && query.changedTraits.has(trait);

        if (hasTracked || hasChanged) trackedIndices.push(i);
        else untrackedIndices.push(i);
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshots(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[],
    state: any[]
) {
    const entityId = getEntityId(entity);
    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
        const trait = traits[i];
        const resolver = pairResolvers[i];
        if (resolver) {
            state[i] = getPairTrackedData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target);
        } else {
            const ctx = trait[$internal];
            state[i] = ctx.get(entityId, stores[i]);
        }
    }
}

/* @inline */ function createSnapshotsWithAtomic(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[],
    state: any[],
    atomicSnapshots: any[]
) {
    const entityId = getEntityId(entity);
    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
        const trait = traits[j];
        const resolver = pairResolvers[j];
        let value: any;
        if (resolver) {
            value = getPairTrackedData(world, entity, resolver.relation, resolver.target);
        } else {
            const ctx = trait[$internal];
            value = ctx.get(entityId, stores[j]);
        }
        state[j] = value;
        atomicSnapshots[j] = value && typeof value === 'object' ? { ...value } : value;
    }
}

/* @inline */ export function getQueryStores<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    params: T,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[],
    world: World
) {
    for (let i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
        const param = params[i];

        // Handle relation pairs
        if (isRelationPair(param)) {
            pushPairResolver(param, traits, stores, pairResolvers, world);
            continue;
        }

        if (isModifier(param)) {
            // Skip not modifier.
            if (param.type === 'not') continue;

            const pairs = param.pairs;
            const modifierTraits = param.traits;

            // Collect pair-backed trait ids so regular traits aren't duplicated.
            const pairTraitIds = new Set<number>();
            if (pairs) {
                for (const pair of pairs) {
                    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
                    const baseTrait = pairCtx.relation[$internal].trait;
                    pairTraitIds.add(baseTrait.id);
                    pushPairResolver(pair, traits, stores, pairResolvers, world);
                }
            }

            for (const trait of modifierTraits) {
                if (pairTraitIds.has(trait.id)) continue; // already pushed via pair resolver
                if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
                traits.push(trait);
                stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
                pairResolvers.push(undefined);
            }
        } else {
            const trait = param as Trait;
            if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') continue; // Skip tags
            traits.push(trait);
            stores.push(getStore(world, trait));
            pairResolvers.push(undefined);
        }
    }
}

/* @inline */ function pushPairResolver(
    pair: RelationPair,
    traits: Trait[],
    stores: Store<any>[],
    pairResolvers: (PairResolver | undefined)[],
    world: World
) {
    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
    const relation = pairCtx.relation as Relation<Trait>;
    const target = pairCtx.target as RelationTarget;
    const baseTrait = relation[$internal].trait;

    if (baseTrait[$internal].type === 'tag') return;

    traits.push(baseTrait);
    stores.push(getStore(world, baseTrait));
    pairResolvers.push(typeof target === 'number' ? { relation, target } : undefined);
}

export function createEmptyQueryResult(): QueryResult<QueryParameter[]> {
    const results = Object.assign([], {
        readEach: () => results,
        updateEach: () => results,
        useStores: () => results,
        select: () => results,
        sort: () => results,
    }) as QueryResult<QueryParameter[]>;

    return results;
}

// Cached no-op result methods for relation-only queries
const relationOnlyMethods = {
    readEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to read, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    updateEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to update, just iterate entities
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    useStores(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No stores, call with empty array
        callback([], this);
        return this;
    },
    select(this: QueryResult<any>) {
        // No-op, nothing to select
        return this;
    },
};

/**
 * Lightweight query result for relation-only queries.
 * Skips store/trait setup since we only need to iterate entities.
 */
export function createRelationOnlyQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    entities: Entity[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach: relationOnlyMethods.readEach,
        updateEach: relationOnlyMethods.updateEach,
        useStores: relationOnlyMethods.useStores,
        select: relationOnlyMethods.select,
        sort(
            callback: (a: Entity, b: Entity) => number = (a, b) => getEntityId(a) - getEntityId(b)
        ): QueryResult<T> {
            Array.prototype.sort.call(entities, callback);
            return results;
        },
    }) as unknown as QueryResult<T>;

    return results;
}

import { createWorld, relation, createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged, Or, $internal } from './src';

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
function assert(cond: any, msg: string) {
  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok:', msg); }
  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL:', msg); }
}

const world = createWorld();
world.init();

// 1. Non-first pair addition
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const bob = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(bob));
  person.add(Likes(alice)); // non-first addition
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'non-first pair addition detected at pair level');
  world.query(Added(Likes(alice))); // drain
  world.reset();
}

// 2. Non-last pair removal
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const bob = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice), Likes(bob));
  person.remove(Likes(alice)); // non-last removal
  const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'non-last pair removal detected at pair level');
  world.query(Removed(Likes(alice))); // drain
  world.reset();
}

// 3. Exclusive replacement produces removal + addition
{
  const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true });
  const Added = createAdded();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const a = world.spawn();
  const b = world.spawn();
  const enemy = world.spawn(Targeting(a));
  world.query(Added(Targeting('*'))); // drain
  world.query(Removed(Targeting('*'))); // drain
  enemy.add(Targeting(b)); // replaces a
  const addQ = world.query(Added(Targeting(b)));
  const remQ = world.query(Removed(Targeting(a)));
  assert(addQ.includes(enemy), 'exclusive replacement fires addition for new pair');
  assert(remQ.includes(enemy), 'exclusive replacement fires removal for old pair');
  world.reset();
}

// 4. Wildcard
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const apple = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(apple));
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes('*')));
  assert(q.includes(person), "wildcard Added(Likes('*')) matches pair addition");
  world.query(Added(Likes('*'))); // drain
  world.reset();
}

// 5. Opposite pair events cancel on same target
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  person.remove(Likes(alice));
  const addQ = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  const remQ = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(addQ.length === 0, 'add+remove cancels Added');
  assert(remQ.length === 0, 'add+remove cancels Removed');
  world.reset();
}

// 6. Entity destruction fires pair-level removal
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice));
  person.destroy();
  const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'entity destruction fires pair-level removal');
  world.reset();
}

// 7. Or composition
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Fears = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const apple = world.spawn();
  const ghost = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(apple));
  const q = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost))));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost))) matches');
  world.query(Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost)))); // drain
  world.reset();
}

// 8. Distinct cached queries per target
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const bob = world.spawn();
  const ctx = world[$internal];
  const qa = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  const qb = world.query(Added(Likes(bob)));
  assert(qa !== qb, 'different pair targets produce distinct cached queries');
  // entities returned separately
  const p1 = world.spawn(Likes(alice));
  const p2 = world.spawn(Likes(bob));
  world.query(Added(Likes(alice))); // drain
  world.query(Added(Likes(bob))); // drain
  world.reset();
}

// 9. Pair modifier + regular trait combined
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Position = { id: 999 } as any; // will use real trait below
  world.reset();
}
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Position = (await import('./src')).trait();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn(Position);
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)), Position);
  assert(q.includes(person), 'pair modifier + regular trait both satisfied');
  world.query(Added(Likes(alice)), Position); // drain

  const person2 = world.spawn();
  person2.add(Likes(alice));
  const q2 = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)), Position);
  assert(!q2.includes(person2), 'pair modifier + regular trait requires regular trait');
  world.reset();
}

// 10. entity.changed accepts RelationPair
{
  const Likes = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const Changed = createChanged();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice, { amount: 1 }));
  world.query(Changed(Likes(alice))); // drain
  person.changed(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'entity.changed(RelationPair) triggers pair change');
  world.reset();
}

// 11. Query result iteration resolves per-target data
{
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const Added = createAdded();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));
  let data: any;
  world.query(Added(Contains(gold))).readEach(([d]: any) => { data = d; });
  assert(data && data.amount === 42, 'Added pair query iteration exposes per-target data');
  world.query(Added(Contains(gold))); // drain
  world.reset();
}
{
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));
  inventory.remove(Contains(gold));
  let data: any;
  const q = world.query(Removed(Contains(gold)));
  assert(q.length === 1, 'Removed pair query returns entity');
  q.readEach(([d]: any) => { data = d; });
  assert(data && data.amount === 42, 'Removed pair query iteration exposes removed pair data');
  world.reset();
}

// 12. Long-lived factories across world reset
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  world.reset();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'tracking modifier works across world reset');
  world.reset();
}

console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

    added: boolean;

old="""    let state = targetMap.get(targetId);
    if (!state) {
        state = { delta: 0, changed: false };
        targetMap.set(targetId, state);
    }

    if (eventType === 'add') state.delta++;
    else if (eventType === 'remove') state.delta--;

new="""    let state = targetMap.get(targetId);
    if (!state) {
        state = { added: false, removed: false, changed: false };
        targetMap.set(targetId, state);
    }

    // Opposite events on the same target cancel each other: an add clears a
    // pending removal and a remove clears a pending addition.
    if (eventType === 'add') {
        state.added = true;
        state.removed = false;
    } else if (eventType === 'remove') {
        state.removed = true;
        state.added = false;
    } else if (eventType === 'change') {
        state.changed = true;
    }

    if (data !== undefined) state.data = data;

    return state;

        }
        return false;

    const state = targetMap.get(pairTarget);
    if (!state) return false;

STEP 86 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='packages/core/src/query/utils/pair-tracking.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# add helper after imports (before updatePairTracker)
helper = """
/**
 * Build a per-generation bitmask of traits that are backed by a relation pair in
 * a tracking group. Those bits are satisfied by pair tracking and should not be
 * required through the trait-level tracker.
 */
export function getGroupPairTraitBitmasks(
    group: { pairs: RelationPair[] },
    world: World
): (number | undefined)[] {
    const result: (number | undefined)[] = [];
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const pair of group.pairs) {
        const pairTrait = pair[$internal].relation[$internal].trait;
        const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, pairTrait);
        if (!instance) continue;
        result[instance.generationId] = (result[instance.generationId] || 0) | instance.bitflag;
    }
    return result;
}

"""
s=s.replace("/**\n * Update (or create) the per-target pair tracker state for a pair event.\n */", helper + "/**\n * Update (or create) the per-target pair tracker state for a pair event.\n */")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("helper added")
PY

helper added

STEP 99 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app/packages/core && cat > tests/pair-tracking-modifiers.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    $internal,
    createAdded,
    createChanged,
    createRemoved,
    createWorld,
    Or,
    relation,
    trait,
    universe,
} from '../src';

describe('Pair tracking modifiers', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    world.init();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('detects non-first pair additions at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Added = createAdded();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const bob = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn();

        person.add(Likes(bob));
        person.add(Likes(alice)); // non-first addition

        const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);

        // Trait-level Added does not fire for non-first additions.
        const traitLevel = world.query(Added(Likes));
        expect(traitLevel).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('detects non-last pair removals at pair level', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const bob = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice), Likes(bob));

        person.remove(Likes(alice)); // non-last removal

        const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);

        // Trait-level Removed does not fire for non-last removals.
        const traitLevel = world.query(Removed(Likes));
        expect(traitLevel).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('wildcard target matches any pair', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        const apple = world.spawn();
        const banana = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn(Likes(apple), Likes(banana));

        person.remove(Likes(apple));

        const q = world.query(Removed(Likes('*')));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);
    });

    it('exclusive replacement produces both a removal and an addition', () => {
        const Targeting = relation({ exclusive: true });
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        const a = world.spawn();
        const b = world.spawn();
        const enemy = world.spawn(Targeting(a));

        // Drain initial pair events.
        world.query(Added(Targeting('*')));
        world.query(Removed(Targeting('*')));

        enemy.add(Targeting(b));

        const added = world.query(Added(Targeting(b)));
        const removed = world.query(Removed(Targeting(a)));
        expect(added).toContain(enemy);
        expect(removed).toContain(enemy);
    });

    it('opposite pair events on the same target cancel Added', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Added = createAdded();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn();

        person.add(Likes(alice));
        person.remove(Likes(alice));

        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(alice)))).toHaveLength(0);
        // A removal event did occur, so Removed still reports it.
        expect(world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)))).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('fires pair-level removal when an entity is destroyed', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice), Likes('*'));

        person.destroy();

        const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);
    });

    it('composes with Or', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Fears = relation();
        const Added = createAdded();

        const apple = world.spawn();
        const ghost = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn();

        person.add(Likes(apple));

        const q = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost))));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);

        // Drain then add the other pair.
        world.query(Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost))));
        person.add(Fears(ghost));
        const q2 = world.query(Or(Added(Likes(apple)), Added(Fears(ghost))));
        expect(q2).toContain(person);
    });

    it('different pair targets produce distinct cached queries', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Added = createAdded();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const bob = world.spawn();

        const qa = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
        const qb = world.query(Added(Likes(bob)));

        expect(qa).not.toBe(qb);

        const ctx = world[$internal];
        // Distinct hashes produce distinct query instances.
        const hashes = [...ctx.queriesHashMap.keys()];
        expect(hashes).toContain((world.query(Added(Likes(alice))) as any).hash);
        expect(hashes).toContain((world.query(Added(Likes(bob))) as any).hash);
    });

    it('combines pair modifiers with regular trait parameters', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Position = trait({ x: 0 });
        const Added = createAdded();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const withPosition = world.spawn(Position);
        const withoutPosition = world.spawn();

        withPosition.add(Likes(alice));
        withoutPosition.add(Likes(alice));

        const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)), Position);
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(withPosition);
        expect(q).not.toContain(withoutPosition);
    });

    it('entity.changed accepts a RelationPair for manual pair-level change signaling', () => {
        const Likes = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const Changed = createChanged();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const bob = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice, { amount: 1 }), Likes(bob, { amount: 2 }));

        // Drain.
        world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)));
        world.query(Changed(Likes(bob)));

        person.changed(Likes(alice));

        const changedAlice = world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)));
        const changedBob = world.query(Changed(Likes(bob)));
        expect(changedAlice).toContain(person);
        expect(changedBob).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('query result iteration resolves per-target relation data', () => {
        const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const Added = createAdded();
        const Removed = createRemoved();

        const gold = world.spawn();
        const inventory = world.spawn();

        inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));

        let addedData: any;
        world.query(Added(Contains(gold))).readEach(([contains]) => {
            addedData = contains;
        });
        expect(addedData).toEqual({ amount: 42 });

        // Drain and remove.
        world.query(Added(Contains(gold)));
        inventory.remove(Contains(gold));

        let removedData: any;
        const removedQuery = world.query(Removed(Contains(gold)));
        expect(removedQuery).toHaveLength(1);
        removedQuery.readEach(([contains]) => {
            removedData = contains;
        });
        expect(removedData).toEqual({ amount: 42 });
    });

    it('tracking modifiers are long-lived and reused across world resets', () => {
        const Likes = relation();
        const Added = createAdded();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        world.reset();

        const person = world.spawn();
        person.add(Likes(alice));

        const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
        expect(q).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(q).toContain(person);
    });

    it('tracks Changed on a relation pair specifically', () => {
        const Likes = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
        const Changed = createChanged();

        const alice = world.spawn();
        const bob = world.spawn();
        const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice, { amount: 1 }), Likes(bob, { amount: 2 }));

        world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)));
        world.query(Changed(Likes(bob)));

        person.set(Likes(alice), { amount: 10 });

        const changedAlice = world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)));
        const changedBob = world.query(Changed(Likes(bob)));
        expect(changedAlice).toContain(person);
        expect(changedBob).toHaveLength(0);
    });
});
EOF

cd /app && cat packages/core/src/query/utils/check-query.ts
OUTPUT:
import { $internal } from '../../common';
import type { Entity } from '../../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import type { World } from '../../world';
import type { QueryInstance } from '../types';

/**
 * Check if an entity matches a non-tracking query.
 * For tracking queries, use checkQueryTracking instead.
 */
export function checkQuery(world: World, query: QueryInstance, entity: Entity): boolean {
    const staticBitmasks = query.staticBitmasks;
    const generations = query.generations;
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

    if (query.traitInstances.all.length === 0) return false;

    for (let i = 0; i < generations.length; i++) {
        const generationId = generations[i];
        const bitmask = staticBitmasks[i];
        if (!bitmask) continue;

        const required = bitmask.required;
        const forbidden = bitmask.forbidden;
        const or = bitmask.or;
        const entityMask = ctx.entityMasks[generationId]?.[eid] || 0;

        if (!forbidden && !required && !or) return false;
        if (forbidden && (entityMask & forbidden) !== 0) return false;
        if (required && (entityMask & required) !== required) return false;
        if (or !== 0 && (entityMask & or) === 0) return false;
    }

    return true;
}

s=open(p).read()
old="""                if (matches) {
                    if (hasRelationFilters) {
                        let relationMatch = true;
                        for (const pair of query.relationFilters!) {
                            if (!hasRelationPair(world, entity, pair)) {
                                relationMatch = false;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (relationMatch) query.add(entity);
                    } else {
                        query.add(entity);
                    }
                }"""
new="""                if (matches) {
                    // Tracking satisfaction alone is not enough: static trait
                    // constraints (required/forbidden/or) and top-level relation
                    // pair filters must be satisfied too.
                    const finalMatch = hasRelationFilters
                        ? checkQueryWithRelations(world, query, entity)
                        : query.check(world, entity);
                    if (finalMatch) query.add(entity);
                }"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

# Fix test 1

        expect(q).toContain(person);

        expect(traitLevel).toHaveLength(0);"""

        const ctx = world[$internal];
        const hashes = [...ctx.queriesHashMap.keys()];
        expect(hashes.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);

        // Sanity: each query only returns its own target's additions.
        world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
        world.query(Added(Likes(bob)));
        const pa = world.spawn(Likes(alice));
        const pb = world.spawn(Likes(bob));
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(alice)))).toContain(pa);
        expect(world.query(Added(Likes(bob)))).toContain(pb);
        world.query(Added(Likes(alice))).forEach((e) => expect(e).toBe(pa));
        world.query(Added(Likes(bob))).forEach((e) => expect(e).toBe(pb));"""

cd /app/packages/core && cat > probe4.ts <<'EOF'
import { createWorld, relation, createRemoved, createAdded, universe } from './src';

let pass=0, fail=0;
const assert=(c:any,m:string)=>{ if(c)pass++; else {fail++; console.log('FAIL',m);} };

// universe.reset + old factory in new world
{
  universe.reset();
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const world = createWorld();
  world.init();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'factory works after universe.reset + new world');
  world.reset();
}

// target destruction fires pair removal on source
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn(Likes(alice));
  world.query(Removed(Likes(alice))); // drain
  alice.destroy();
  const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'target destruction fires pair removal on source');
  world.reset();
}

// Added query created before add, then add + remove cancels Added
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Added = createAdded();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  world.query(Added(Likes(alice))); // create + drain
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  person.remove(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.length === 0, 'incremental add+remove cancels Added');
  world.reset();
}

// Removed query created before events, add then remove -> Removed matches
{
  const Likes = relation();
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const alice = world.spawn();
  const person = world.spawn();
  world.query(Removed(Likes(alice))); // create + drain
  person.add(Likes(alice));
  person.remove(Likes(alice));
  const q = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)));
  assert(q.includes(person), 'incremental add+remove records Removed');
  world.reset();
}

console.log(`${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
EOF

import { createWorld, relation, createRemoved, createAdded } from './src';

const world = createWorld();
world.init();

// Removed relation updateEach now actually resolves per-target data
{
  const Removed = createRemoved();
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const inventory = world.spawn();
  const gold = world.spawn();
  inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }));
  inventory.remove(Contains(gold));

  let ran = 0;
  world.query(Removed(Contains(gold))).updateEach(([contains]: any, entity: any) => {
    ran++;
    console.log('removed callback:', contains, 'target:', entity.targetFor(Contains));
  });
  console.log('callback ran', ran);
}

// Added relation updateEach commits per-target changes
{
  const Added = createAdded();
  const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
  const gold = world.spawn();
  const inventory = world.spawn(Contains(gold, { amount: 1 }));
  world.query(Added(Contains(gold))); // drain
  world.query(Added(Contains(gold))).updateEach(([contains]: any, entity: any) => {
    contains.amount = 99;
  });
  console.log('after updateEach amount', inventory.get(Contains(gold)));
  const Changed = createChanged();
  console.log('Changed pair matches:', world.query(Changed(Contains(gold))).length);
}
EOF

---
title: Entity
description: Entity API
nav: 3
---

An entity is a number encoded with a world, generation and ID. Every entity is unique even if they have the same ID since they will have different generations. This makes automatic-recycling possible without reference errors. Because of this, the number of an entity won't give you its ID but will have to instead be decoded with `entity.id()`.

```js
// Add a trait to the entity
entity.add(Position)

// Remove a trait from the entity
entity.remove(Position)

// Checks if the entity has the trait
// Return boolean
const result = entity.has(Position)

// Gets the trait record for an entity
// Return TraitRecord
const position = entity.get(Position)

// Sets the trait and triggers a change event
entity.set(Position, { x: 10, y: 10 })
// Can take a callback with the previous state passed in
entity.set(Position, (prev) => ({
  x: prev + 1,
  y: prev + 1,
}))

// Get the targets for a relation
// Return Entity[]
const targets = entity.targetsFor(Contains)

// Get the first target for a relation
// Return Entity
const target = entity.targetFor(Contains)

// Get the entity ID
// Return number
const id = entity.id()

// Get the entity generation
// Return number
const generation = entity.generation()

// Destroys the entity making its number no longer valid
entity.destroy()
```

For introspection, `unpackEntity` can be used to get all of the encoded values. This can be useful for debugging.

```js
const { entityId, generation, worldId } = unpackEntity(entity)=====SKILL QUERIES=====
# Queries

Complete guide to querying entities in Koota.

## Contents

- [Basic queries](#basic-queries)
- [Query modifiers](#query-modifiers) - Not, Or
- [Tracking modifiers](#tracking-modifiers) - Added, Removed, Changed
- [Caching queries](#caching-queries) - createQuery for performance
- [Change detection](#change-detection) - updateEach options
- [Query + select](#query--select) - Select subset of traits for updates
- [Direct store access](#direct-store-access) - useStores for performance

## Basic queries

Queries fetch entities that share specific traits (archetypes).

```typescript
// Returns QueryResult (Entity[] with extra methods)
const entities = world.query(Position, Velocity)

// Batch update with updateEach
world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([pos, vel]) => {
  pos.x += vel.x
  pos.y += vel.y
})

// Batch read with readEach
world.query(Position).readEach(([pos], entity) => {
  // ...
})

// Get first match only
const player = world.queryFirst(IsPlayer, Position)

// Query all entities (excludes system entities)
const allEntities = world.query()
```

## Query modifiers

Filter queries with logical modifiers.

```typescript
import { Not, Or } from 'koota'

// Has Position but NOT Velocity
world.query(Position, Not(Velocity))

// Has IsPlayer OR IsEnemy
world.query(Or(IsPlayer, IsEnemy))

// Combine modifiers
world.query(Position, Not(Velocity), Or(IsPlayer, IsEnemy))
```

## Tracking modifiers

Track structural and data changes. Each tracking modifier must be created as a unique instance.

```typescript
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

// Create unique instances (typically at module scope)
const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()
```

**Added** - Entities that added a trait since last query:

```typescript
const newPositions = world.query(Added(Position))

// Track relation additions
const newChildren = world.query(Added(ChildOf))
```

**Removed** - Entities that removed a trait since last query (includes destroyed entities):

```typescript
const stoppedEntities = world.query(Removed(Velocity))

// Track orphaned entities
const orphaned = world.query(Removed(ChildOf))
```

**Changed** - Entities whose trait data changed since last query:

```typescript
const movedEntities = world.query(Changed(Position))

// Track relation data changes
const updatedChildren = world.query(Changed(ChildOf))
```

**Logical AND (default):**

When multiple traits are passed to a tracking modifier, it uses logical AND. Only entities where **all** specified traits match the condition are returned:

```typescript
// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were added
const fullyAdded = world.query(Added(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity were removed
const fullyRemoved = world.query(Removed(Position, Velocity))

// Entities where BOTH Position AND Velocity have changed
const fullyUpdated = world.query(Changed(Position, Velocity))
```

**Logical OR:**

To track entities where **any** of the specified traits match, wrap individual tracking modifiers in `Or()`:

```typescript
import { Or } from 'koota'

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was added
const eitherAdded = world.query(Or(Added(Position), Added(Velocity)))

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity was removed
const eitherRemoved = world.query(Or(Removed(Position), Removed(Velocity)))

// Entities where EITHER Position OR Velocity has changed
const eitherChanged = world.query(Or(Changed(Position), Changed(Velocity)))
```

**Key points:**

- Create instances at module scope, not inside functions
- Tracking resets after each query execution
- Changed only tracks `set()` calls and `entity.changed()` signals

## Caching queries

Inline queries hash parameters each call. For hot paths, cache with `createQuery`.

```typescript
import { createQuery } from 'koota'

// Define once at module scope
const movementQuery = createQuery(Position, Velocity)

function updateMovement(world: World) {
  // Fast array-based lookup
  world.query(movementQuery).updateEach(([pos, vel]) => {
    pos.x += vel.x
    pos.y += vel.y
  })
}
```

**When to use:**

- Systems called every frame
- Queries in tight loops
- Performance-critical code

**When inline is fine:**

- Event handlers
- Startup/cleanup code
- Infrequent operations

## Change detection

`updateEach` automatically detects changes for traits tracked via `onChange` or `Changed` modifier.

```typescript
// Default: selective detection (only tracked traits)
world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([pos, vel]) => {
  pos.x += vel.x
})

// Never trigger change events (silent updates)
world.query(Position).updateEach(
  ([pos]) => {
    pos.x += 1
  },
  { changeDetection: 'never' }
)

// Always trigger change events for all mutated traits (DEFAULT)
world.query(Position).updateEach(
  ([pos]) => {
    pos.x += 1
  },
  { changeDetection: 'always' }
)
```

**Shallow comparison:**

Change detection uses shallow comparison like React. Objects and arrays only detect changes if replaced:

```typescript
// ❌ Mutation not detected (same array reference)
world.query(Inventory).updateEach(([inv]) => {

new="""## Relation pair tracking

Tracking modifiers accept relation pairs so you can react to changes for a specific target. The `'*'` wildcard matches any target.

```js
import { createAdded, createRemoved, createChanged } from 'koota'

const Added = createAdded()
const Removed = createRemoved()
const Changed = createChanged()

// Track when an entity starts liking a specific target
const newLikes = world.query(Added(Likes(alice)))

// Track when an entity stops liking a specific target
const removedLikes = world.query(Removed(Likes(alice)))

// Wildcard target tracks any pair
const anyNewLikes = world.query(Added(Likes('*')))

// Track data changes for a specific relation pair
const updatedLikes = world.query(Changed(Likes(alice)))
```

Pair-level tracking is per-target:

- Non-first pair additions (when the entity already targets another entity) are detected.
- Non-last pair removals (when the entity still targets another entity) are detected.
- Replacing an exclusive relation produces both a removal of the old pair and an addition of the new pair.
- Entity destruction fires pair-level removals for all active pairs.
- Opposite add/remove events on the same target cancel: an add followed by a remove no longer reports `Added` for that pair.
- Pair modifiers compose with `Or`: `world.query(Or(Added(Likes(alice)), Added(Fears(ghost))))`.
- Pair modifiers combined with regular traits must satisfy all constraints: `world.query(Added(Likes(alice)), Position)` only returns entities that added the pair AND have `Position`.

Query result iteration resolves per-target data for pair-tracked traits:

```js
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } })
const Added = createAdded()

inventory.add(Contains(gold, { amount: 42 }))

world.query(Added(Contains(gold))).readEach(([contains]) => {
  console.log(contains.amount) // 42 (per-target, not the raw store array)
})
```

The `entity.changed()` method also accepts a `RelationPair` for manual pair-level change signaling:

```js
entity.changed(Likes(alice))
```

## Add, remove and change events"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
